## User

I want to create a netflix kind clone compatible with my android tv with the stack, nuxt 4, supabase,vercel, the source of the videos will be via this API Get movie embed URL:
    Endpoint: https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/movie

    Valid parameters:
    imdb or tmdb required - from imdb.com or themoviedb.com
    sub_url optional - Url encoded .srt or .vtt url. Must have CORS enabled to the URL.
    ds_lang optional - Default subtitle language, ISO639 Language code.
    autoplay optional - 1 or 0. Enable or Disable autoplay. (Enabled default)

    Examples
    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/movie/tt5433140

    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/movie?imdb=tt5433140

    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/movie?imdb=tt5433140&ds_lang=de

    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/movie?imdb=tt5433140&sub_url=https%3A%2F%2Fvidsrc.me%2Fsample.srt&autoplay=1

    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/movie/385687

    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/movie?tmdb=385687/

    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/movie?tmdb=385687&ds_lang=de

    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/movie?tmdb=385687&sub_url=https%3A%2F%2Fvidsrc.me%2Fsample.srt&autoplay=1

    Get tv show embed URL:
    Endpoint: https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/tv

    Valid parameters:
    imdb or tmdb required - from imdb.com or themoviedb.com
    ds_lang optional - Default subtitle language, ISO639 Language code.

    Examples
    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/tv/tt0944947

    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/tv?imdb=tt0944947

    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/tv?imdb=tt0944947&ds_lang=de

    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/tv/1399

    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/tv?tmdb=1399&ds_lang=de

    Get episode embed URL:
    Endpoint: https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/tv

    Valid parameters:
    imdb or tmdb required - from imdb.com or themoviedb.com
    season required - the season number.
    episode required - the season episode.
    sub_url optional - Url encoded .srt or .vtt url. Must have CORS enabled to the URL.
    ds_lang optional - Default subtitle language, ISO639 Language code.
    autoplay optional - 1 or 0. Enable or Disable autoplay. (Enabled default)
    autonext optional - 1 or 0. Enable or Disable autonext episode. (Disabled default)

    Examples
    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/tv/tt0944947/1-1

    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/tv?imdb=tt0944947&season=1&episode=1

    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/tv?imdb=tt0944947&season=1&episode=1ds_lang=de

    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/tv?imdb=tt0944947&season=1&episode=1&sub_url=https%3A%2F%2Fvidsrc.me%2Fsample.srt&autoplay=1&autonext=1

    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/tv/1399/1-1

    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/tv?tmdb=1399&season=1&episode=1

    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/tv?tmdb=1399&season=1&episode=1&ds_lang=de

    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/tv?tmdb=1399&season=1&episode=1&sub_url=https%3A%2F%2Fvidsrc.me%2Fsample.srt&autoplay=1&autonext=1

    List latest movies added:

    PAGE_NUMBER required
    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/movies/latest/page-PAGE_NUMBER.json

    Examples
    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/movies/latest/page-1.json

    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/movies/latest/page-15.json

    List latest tv shows added:

    PAGE_NUMBER required
    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/tvshows/latest/page-PAGE_NUMBER.json

    Examples
    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/tvshows/latest/page-1.json

    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/tvshows/latest/page-15.json

    List latest episodes added:

    PAGE_NUMBER required
    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/episodes/latest/page-PAGE_NUMBER.json

    Examples
    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/episodes/latest/page-1.json

    https://vidsrc-embed.ru/episodes/latest/page-25.json for finding shows using the search bar we can rely on tmdb, users need to be able to keep track of their view history and continue from already watched

# Netflix Clone for Android TV — Plan

## Tech Stack
| Layer | Technology |
|-------|-----------|
| Frontend | **Nuxt 4** (SPA mode for TV — no SSR needed, faster client-side navigation) |
| Backend/DB | **Supabase** (PostgreSQL + Auth + Realtime) |
| Video Source | **vidsrc-embed.ru** (iframe embed) |
| Metadata/Search | **TMDB API** (free tier, 25 req/s) |
| Deployment | **Vercel** (auto HTTPS, edge functions for API proxy) |

## Database Schema (Supabase)

```sql
-- Users profile (extends auth.users)
CREATE TABLE profiles (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  email TEXT,
  display_name TEXT,
  avatar_url TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  updated_at TIMESTAMTS DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Watch history — tracks everything the user watches
CREATE TABLE watch_history (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id UUID REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  tmdb_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
  media_type TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (media_type IN ('movie', 'tv')),
  title TEXT NOT NULL,
  poster_url TEXT,
  backdrop_url TEXT,
  progress_seconds INTEGER DEFAULT 0,      -- where they left off
  total_seconds INTEGER DEFAULT 0,         -- total duration
  percentage_complete REAL DEFAULT 0,      -- 0-100
  season_number INTEGER,                   -- for TV episodes only
  episode_number INTEGER,                  -- for TV episodes only
  last_watched_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  UNIQUE(user_id, tmdb_id, season_number, episode_number)
);

-- Create indexes for fast queries
CREATE INDEX idx_watch_history_user ON watch_history(user_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_watch_history_type ON watch_history(tmdb_id, media_type);

-- Row Level Security
ALTER TABLE watch_history ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Users can only see their own history
CREATE POLICY "Users can view own watch history"
  ON watch_history FOR SELECT
  USING (auth.uid() = user_id);

CREATE POLICY "Users can insert own watch history"
  ON watch_history FOR INSERT
  WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

CREATE POLICY "Users can update own watch history"
  ON watch_history FOR UPDATE
  USING (auth.uid() = user_id);

CREATE POLICY "Users can delete own watch history"
  ON watch_history FOR DELETE
  USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
```

---

## Page-by-Page Breakdown

### 1. **Home Page** (`/`)
- **Hero Banner**: Rotating featured/trending movies & shows with backdrop images
- **Carousel Rows** (horizontal scroll with focus navigation):
  - Continue Watching (with progress bar)
  - Trending Now
  - Popular Movies
  - Popular TV Shows
  - Top Rated
  - New Releases
- Each row uses `TvCarousel` component with left/right arrow navigation via D-pad

### 2. **Sign In / Sign Up** (`/signin`, `/signup`)
- Email + password form with Supabase Auth
- "Remember me" option
- Social login (Google) via Supabase providers
- Redirect to `/` after successful auth

### 3. **Search** (`/search?q=query`)
- Full-screen search overlay (triggered by focus on search bar)
- Queries TMDB's search API
- Results grid with movie/show cards
- Supports filtering by type (movie, tv, person)
- Type-ahead debounced search

### 4. **Movie Detail** (`/movie-[id]`)
- Full backdrop image header
- Title, year, rating, genre badges
- Synopsis
- "Play" button → navigates to `/watch?tmdb={id}&type=movie`
- Cast section
- "Add to My List" action
- Similar movies carousel

### 5. **TV Show Detail** (`/tv-[id]`)
- Similar to movie detail
- Lists seasons as selectable cards
- Each season shows episodes
- Clicking episode → `/watch?tmdb={id}&type=tv&season={s}&episode={e}`

### 6. **Video Player** (`/watch`)
- Full-screen iframe embedding vidsrc-embed.ru
- URL construction:
  - Movie: `https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/movie/{tmdb_id}`
  - TV: `https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/tv/{tmdb_id}/{season}-{episode}`
- Supports subtitle language param (`ds_lang`)
- Progress tracking:
  - Sends progress updates to Supabase every 10 seconds via `useWatchHistory` composable
  - On page leave, saves current position
  - If user returns, resumes from last position
- Back button returns to detail page

### 7. **My List** (`/my-list`)
- Tabbed interface:
  - **Continue Watching** — shows items with progress > 0, sorted by last_watched
  - **Watch History** — chronological list of everything watched
  - **My List** — saved/favorited items
- Each item shows poster, title, and a progress bar
- Clicking resumes playback at saved position

---

## Android TV UX Details

### D-Pad Navigation System
```
composable: useFocusManager.ts

Key mappings:
├── D-Pad Up/Down/Left/Right    → Navigate focus between elements
├── D-Pad Center (Enter)        → Activate focused element (click)
├── Back                        → Go to previous page / close modal
├── Play/Pause                  → Toggle video playback
├── Menu                        → Open context menu (if needed)
└── Search button               → Open search overlay
```

### Focus Management Rules
- Every interactive element gets `tabindex="0"`
- `TvFocusRing` component provides visible focus state (white border + glow)
- Focus manager uses a focus ring system (like Android TV's leanback)
- Auto-scroll to bring focused element into view
- First focus on page load: logo or primary CTA

### TV-Specific Styling
- Minimum font: `16px` body, `24px` headings
- Card minimum height: `200px` (portrait) / `120px` (landscape)
- Touch target: minimum `48px`
- High contrast mode for text
- Dark theme only (no light mode needed for TV)

## API Routes (Server-Side Proxy)

All TMDB calls go through the server to protect the API key:

```
GET /api/tmdb/trending          → fetches trending from TMDB
GET /api/tmdb/search?q=...      → searches TMDB
GET /api/tmdb/movie/[id]        → gets movie details
GET /api/tmdb/tv/[id]           → gets TV show details
GET /api/tmdb/tv/[id]/season/[n] → gets season details
```

---

## Environment Variables

```env
# .env
VITE_SUPABASE_URL=your_supabase_url
VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=your_anon_key
VITE_TMDB_API_KEY=your_tmdb_api_key
VITE_TMDB_LANGUAGE=en
VITE_VIDSRC_BASE_URL=https://vidsrc-embed.ru
```

---

## Deployment on Vercel

```json
{
  "buildCommand": "nuxt build",
  "devCommand": "nuxt dev",
  "outputDirectory": ".output/server",
  "framework": "nuxt",
  "rewrites": [
    { "source": "/(.*)", "destination": "/" }
  ]
}
```

---

## Implementation Order

| Phase | Tasks | Estimated Effort |
|-------|-------|-----------------|
| **1. Setup** | Nuxt 4 init, Supabase project, TMDB API key, env config, basic layout | 1-2 hours |
| **2. Auth** | Sign in/up pages, Supabase auth integration, protected routes | 2 hours |
| **3. Home Page** | Hero banner, carousels, fetch trending/popular from TMDB | 3 hours |
| **4. Detail Pages** | Movie/TV detail layouts, season/episode listing | 3 hours |
| **5. Video Player** | iframe embed, vidsrc URL construction, fullscreen | 2 hours |
| **6. Watch History** | Supabase schema, progress tracking composable, "continue watching" | 3 hours |
| **7. Search** | TMDB search, results page, search overlay | 2 hours |
| **8. My List** | History page, progress bars, resume playback | 2 hours |
| **9. Android TV UX** | Focus manager, D-pad navigation, focus rings, responsive sizing | 3 hours |
| **10. Polish** | Loading states, error handling, animations, testing on TV | 2-3 hours |

**Total: ~23-25 hours**

---

## Risks & Considerations

1. **vidsrc-embed.ru availability** — This is a third-party service. It could go down or change its API. Consider adding a fallback embed source later.
2. **TMDB API rate limits** — Free tier allows 25 req/s. Server-side caching in Supabase or Redis would help at scale.
3. **CORS on vidsrc-embed.ru** — If they block iframe embedding, we may need a proxy. Vercel edge functions can handle this.
4. **Android TV testing** — You'll need an actual Android TV device or the Android TV emulator to test D-pad navigation properly.
5. **Content legality** — Ensure vidsrc-embed.ru complies with copyright laws in your region.

````diff
# Pichisfin - Netflix Clone for Android TV

## Project Overview
A Netflix-style streaming application optimized for Android TV, built with Nuxt 4, Supabase, and Vercel. Video content is streamed via vidsrc-embed.ru with metadata from TMDB API.

## Tech Stack
- **Frontend**: Nuxt 4 (SPA mode)
- **Backend**: Supabase (PostgreSQL + Auth + Realtime)
- **Video Source**: vidsrc-embed.ru (iframe embed)
- **Metadata**: TMDB API
- **Deployment**: Vercel
- **Styling**: TailwindCSS + custom TV-focused UI

## Architecture

### Core Features
1. **User Authentication** (Email/Password + Google)
2. **Content Browsing** (Trending, Popular, Categories)
3. **Search** (TMDB-powered)
4. **Video Player** (iframe embed with progress tracking)
5. **Watch History** (Continue watching, progress tracking)
6. **Android TV Navigation** (D-pad, focus management)

### Directory Structure
```
pichisfin/
├── src/
│   ├── app.vue
│   ├── assets/css/
│   ├── components/
│   │   ├── tv/          # TV-specific components
│   │   ├── common/      # Shared components
│   │   ├── auth/        # Auth forms
│   │   ├── player/      # Video player
│   │   └── search/      # Search UI
│   ├── composables/     # Logic hooks
│   ├── layouts/         # Page layouts
│   ├── pages/           # Route pages
│   ├── server/api/      # API proxy routes
│   └── types/           # TypeScript definitions
├── supabase/
│   └── migrations/      # Database schema
├── .env.example
└── vercel.json
```

## Implementation Plan

### Phase 1: Project Setup (1-2 hours)
- [ ] Initialize Nuxt 4 project
- [ ] Configure TailwindCSS
- [ ] Set up Supabase project
- [ ] Configure environment variables
- [ ] Create basic layout structure
- [ ] Set up TypeScript types

### Phase 2: Authentication (2 hours)
- [ ] Create Supabase auth schema
- [ ] Build sign-in/sign-up pages
- [ ] Implement auth composable
- [ ] Add protected routes
- [ ] Configure session persistence

### Phase 3: Content Discovery (3 hours)
- [ ] Create TMDB API client composable
- [ ] Build home page with hero banner
- [ ] Implement content carousels
- [ ] Add trending/popular sections
- [ ] Create category browsing

### Phase 4: Detail Pages (3 hours)
- [ ] Movie detail page
- [ ] TV show detail page
- [ ] Season/episode selection
- [ ] Cast and credits display
- [ ] Similar content recommendations

### Phase 5: Video Player (2 hours)
- [ ] Create video player page
- [ ] Implement iframe embed logic
- [ ] Add fullscreen support
- [ ] Handle subtitle options
- [ ] Add playback controls

### Phase 6: Watch History (3 hours)
- [ ] Create watch history schema
- [ ] Implement progress tracking composable
- [ ] Build "Continue Watching" section
- [ ] Add resume playback functionality
- [ ] Create my list page

### Phase 7: Search (2 hours)
- [ ] Build search overlay
- [ ] Implement TMDB search integration
- [ ] Create search results page
- [ ] Add type-ahead suggestions
- [ ] Filter by content type

### Phase 8: Android TV Optimization (3 hours)
- [ ] Implement D-pad navigation
- [ ] Create focus management system
- [ ] Add TV-specific styling
- [ ] Optimize for 10-foot UX
- [ ] Test on Android TV emulator

### Phase 9: Polish & Testing (2-3 hours)
- [ ] Add loading states
- [ ] Implement error handling
- [ ] Add animations
- [ ] Performance optimization
- [ ] Cross-device testing

## Database Schema

### Profiles Table
```sql
CREATE TABLE profiles (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES auth.users(id),
  email TEXT,
  display_name TEXT,
  avatar_url TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
```

### Watch History Table
```sql
CREATE TABLE watch_history (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id UUID REFERENCES auth.users(id),
  tmdb_id INTEGER,
  media_type TEXT,
  title TEXT,
  poster_url TEXT,
  progress_seconds INTEGER,
  total_seconds INTEGER,
  percentage_complete REAL,
  season_number INTEGER,
  episode_number INTEGER,
  last_watched_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
```

## API Endpoints

### Server Routes (Nuxt)
- `/api/tmdb/trending` - Get trending content
- `/api/tmdb/search` - Search TMDB
- `/api/tmdb/movie/[id]` - Movie details
- `/api/tmdb/tv/[id]` - TV show details
- `/api/tmdb/tv/[id]/season/[n]` - Season details

### External APIs
- **TMDB**: `https://api.themoviedb.org/3/`
- **VidSrc**: `https://vidsrc-embed.ru/embed/`

## Android TV UX Guidelines
- **Navigation**: D-pad arrow keys
- **Focus States**: Visible white border + glow
- **Font Sizes**: Minimum 16px body, 24px headings
- **Touch Targets**: Minimum 48px
- **Layout**: 10-foot optimized spacing
- **Theme**: Dark mode only

## Deployment
- **Platform**: Vercel
- **Build**: `nuxt build`
- **Environment**: Supabase + TMDB API keys
- **HTTPS**: Automatic via Vercel
- **CDN**: Edge caching for static assets

## Risks & Mitigations
1. **vidsrc-embed.ru availability** - Add fallback sources later
2. **TMDB rate limits** - Implement server-side caching
3. **CORS issues** - Use Vercel edge functions as proxy
4. **Android TV testing** - Use emulator + real device testing

## Next Steps
1. Initialize Nuxt 4 project
2. Set up Supabase database
3. Get TMDB API key
4. Begin Phase 1 implementation

// https://nuxt.com/docs/api/configuration/nuxt-config
export default defineNuxtConfig({
  // Disable SSR for better TV app performance (SPA mode)
  ssr: false,

  // Compatibility date for Nuxt 4
  compatibilityDate: '2025-01-01',

  // App configuration
  app: {
    head: {
      title: 'Pichisfin - Stream Movies & TV Shows',
      meta: [
        { name: 'viewport', content: 'width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, maximum-scale=1.0, user-scalable=no' },
        { name: 'description', content: 'Stream your favorite movies and TV shows on Android TV' },
        { name: 'theme-color', content: '#141414' },
      ],
      link: [
        { rel: 'icon', type: 'image/svg+xml', href: '/favicon.svg' },
      ],
    },
  },

  // Modules
  modules: [
    '@nuxt/image',
  ],

  // CSS
  css: ['~/assets/css/tailwind.css'],

  // PostCSS
  postcss: {
    plugins: {
      tailwindcss: {},
      autoprefixer: {},
    },
  },

  // Runtime config for environment variables
  runtimeConfig: {
    public: {
      supabaseUrl: process.env.NUXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL || '',
      supabaseAnonKey: process.env.NUXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY || '',
      tmdbApiKey: process.env.NUXT_PUBLIC_TMDB_API_KEY || '',
      tmdbLanguage: process.env.NUXT_PUBLIC_TMDB_LANGUAGE || 'en',
      vidsrcBaseUrl: process.env.NUXT_PUBLIC_VIDSRC_BASE_URL || 'https://vidsrc-embed.ru',
    },
  },

  // TypeScript
  typescript: {
    strict: false,
  },

  // Nitro server configuration

    compressPublicAssets: true,
  },

  // Image module configuration

    domains: [
      'image.tmdb.org',
    ],
    alias: {
      tmdb: 'https://image.tmdb.org',
    },
  },

  // Devtools
  devtools: { enabled: true },
})

/** @type {import('tailwindcss').Config} */
export default {
  content: [
    './src/**/*.{vue,js,ts,jsx,tsx}',
  ],
  darkMode: 'class',
  theme: {
    extend: {
      colors: {
        // Netflix-inspired color palette
        netflix: {
          red: '#E50914',
          darkRed: '#B20710',
          black: '#141414',
          dark: '#181818',
          gray: '#808080',
          lightGray: '#B3B3B3',
          white: '#E5E5E5',
        },
      },
      fontFamily: {
        sans: ['Inter', 'system-ui', '-apple-system', 'sans-serif'],
      },
      fontSize: {
        // TV-optimized font sizes (larger for 10-foot UX)
        'xs': '14px',
        'sm': '16px',
        'base': '18px',
        'lg': '20px',
        'xl': '24px',
        '2xl': '30px',
        '3xl': '36px',
        '4xl': '48px',
        '5xl': '60px',
      },
      spacing: {
        // TV-optimized spacing
        'tv-sm': '8px',
        'tv-md': '16px',
        'tv-lg': '24px',
        'tv-xl': '32px',
        'tv-2xl': '48px',
      },
      borderRadius: {
        'none': '0',
        'sm': '4px',
        'DEFAULT': '8px',
        'lg': '12px',
        'xl': '16px',
        'full': '9999px',
      },
      screens: {
        // Android TV optimized breakpoints
        'sm': '640px',
        'md': '768px',
        'lg': '1024px',
        'xl': '1280px',  // Standard Android TV resolution
        '2xl': '1920px', // Full HD Android TV
      },
      animation: {
        'fade-in': 'fadeIn 0.3s ease-in-out',
        'slide-up': 'slideUp 0.3s ease-out',
        'slide-down': 'slideDown 0.3s ease-out',
        'scale-in': 'scaleIn 0.2s ease-out',
      },
      keyframes: {
        fadeIn: {
          '0%': { opacity: '0' },
          '100%': { opacity: '1' },
        },
        slideUp: {
          '0%': { transform: 'translateY(20px)', opacity: '0' },
          '100%': { transform: 'translateY(0)', opacity: '1' },
        },
        slideDown: {
          '0%': { transform: 'translateY(-20px)', opacity: '0' },
          '100%': { transform: 'translateY(0)', opacity: '1' },
        },
        scaleIn: {
          '0%': { transform: 'scale(0.95)', opacity: '0' },
          '100%': { transform: 'scale(1)', opacity: '1' },
        },
      },
    },
  },
  plugins: [
    require('@tailwindcss/forms'),
    require('@tailwindcss/typography'),
  ],
}

    font-size: 16px;
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
    -moz-osx-font-smoothing: grayscale;
  }

  body {
    @apply bg-netflix-black;
    min-height: 100vh;
    overflow-x: hidden;
  }

  /* Custom scrollbar for TV */
  ::-webkit-scrollbar {
    width: 8px;
    height: 8px;
  }

  ::-webkit-scrollbar-track {
    @apply bg-netflix-dark;
  }

  ::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
    @apply bg-netflix-gray rounded;
  }

  ::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover {
    @apply bg-netflix-lightGray;
  }
}

@layer components {
  /* TV Focus Ring */
  .tv-focus {
    @apply outline-none;
    transition: all 0.15s ease-in-out;
  }

  .tv-focus:focus {
    @apply ring-4 ring-white ring-opacity-80;
    transform: scale(1.05);
    box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.3);
  }

  /* Focus ring for cards */
  .tv-card-focus {
    @apply outline-none;
    transition: all 0.2s ease-in-out;
  }

  .tv-card-focus:focus {
    transform: scale(1.08);
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
    z-index: 10;
  }

  /* Hero gradient overlay */
  .hero-gradient {
    background: linear-gradient(
      to top,
      rgba(20, 20, 20, 1) 0%,
      rgba(20, 20, 20, 0.8) 30%,
      rgba(20, 20, 20, 0.4) 50%,
      rgba(20, 20, 20, 0.2) 70%,
      rgba(20, 20, 20, 0) 100%
    );
  }

  /* Card backdrop gradient */
  .card-gradient {
    background: linear-gradient(
      to top,
      rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9) 0%,
      rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5) 50%,
      rgba(0, 0, 0, 0) 100%
    );
  }

  /* Netflix-style button */
  .btn-primary {
    @apply bg-netflix-red hover:bg-netflix-darkRed text-white font-semibold;
    @apply px-6 py-3 rounded transition-all duration-200;
    @apply focus:ring-4 focus:ring-netflix-red focus:ring-opacity-50;
  }

  .btn-secondary {
    @apply bg-gray-600 bg-opacity-70 hover:bg-opacity-50 text-white font-semibold;
    @apply px-6 py-3 rounded transition-all duration-200;
    @apply focus:ring-4 focus:ring-white focus:ring-opacity-50;
  }

  /* Carousel navigation */
  .carousel-btn {
    @apply bg-netflix-dark bg-opacity-80 hover:bg-opacity-100;
    @apply text-white p-4 rounded transition-all duration-200;
    @apply focus:ring-4 focus:ring-white focus:ring-opacity-50;
  }

  /* Progress bar */
  .progress-bar {
    @apply h-1 bg-netflix-gray rounded;
    overflow: hidden;
  }

  .progress-bar-fill {
    @apply h-full bg-netflix-red rounded;
    transition: width 0.3s ease-in-out;
  }

  /* Skeleton loading */
  .skeleton {
    @apply bg-netflix-dark animate-pulse rounded;
    background: linear-gradient(
      90deg,
      rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05) 25%,
      rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1) 50%,
      rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05) 75%
    );
    background-size: 200% 100%;
    animation: shimmer 1.5s infinite;
  }

  @keyframes shimmer {
    0% {
      background-position: 200% 0;
    }
    100% {
      background-position: -200% 0;
    }
  }

  /* Modal overlay */
  .modal-overlay {
    @apply fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-75 z-50;
    backdrop-filter: blur(4px);
  }

  /* Genre badge */
  .genre-badge {
    @apply px-3 py-1 bg-gray-700 bg-opacity-60 rounded-full text-sm;
  }

  /* Rating badge */
  .rating-badge {
    @apply px-2 py-1 bg-yellow-500 bg-opacity-90 text-black font-bold rounded text-sm;
  }
}

/* Hide scrollbar on carousel containers but allow scrolling */
.carousel-container {
  scrollbar-width: none; /* Firefox */
  -ms-overflow-style: none; /* IE and Edge */
}

.carousel-container::-webkit-scrollbar {
  display: none; /* Chrome, Safari, Opera */
}

```

*Diff for `useAuth.ts`:*
```diff
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'

export function useAuth() {
  const supabase = useSupabase()
  const router = useRouter()
  
  const isAuthenticated = ref(false)
  const currentUser = ref<any>(null)
  const loading = ref(true)

  // Initialize auth state
  onMounted(async () => {
    await checkSession()
  })

  async function checkSession() {
    try {
      const { data: { session }, error } = await supabase.auth.getSession()
      
      if (error) throw error
      
      isAuthenticated.value = !!session
      currentUser.value = session?.user || null
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error checking session:', error)
      isAuthenticated.value = false
      currentUser.value = null
    } finally {
      loading.value = false
    }
  }

  async function signIn(email: string, password: string) {
    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({
        email,
        password,
      })

      if (error) throw error

      isAuthenticated.value = true
      currentUser.value = data.user
      return { success: true, user: data.user }
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error('Sign in error:', error)
      return { success: false, error: error.message }
    }
  }

  async function signUp(email: string, password: string, displayName?: string) {
    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signUp({
        email,
        password,
        options: {
          data: {
            display_name: displayName || null,
          },
        },
      })

      if (error) throw error

      // Create profile in profiles table
      if (data.user) {
        await supabase.from('profiles').insert({
          id: data.user.id,
          email: data.user.email,
          display_name: displayName || null,
        })
      }

      return { success: true, user: data.user }
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error('Sign up error:', error)
      return { success: false, error: error.message }
    }
  }

  async function signInWithGoogle() {
    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithOAuth({
        provider: 'google',
        options: {
          redirectTo: window.location.origin,
        },
      })

      if (error) throw error
      return { success: true }
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error('Google sign in error:', error)
      return { success: false, error: error.message }
    }
  }

  async function signOut() {
    try {
      const { error } = await supabase.auth.signOut()
      
      if (error) throw error

      isAuthenticated.value = false
      currentUser.value = null
      await router.push('/signin')
      
      return { success: true }
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error('Sign out error:', error)
      return { success: false, error: error.message }
    }
  }

  return {
    isAuthenticated,
    currentUser,
    loading,
    checkSession,
    signIn,
    signUp,
    signInWithGoogle,
    signOut,
  }
}

```

'~/types/tmdb'\n\nexport function useTmdb() {\n  const config = useRuntimeConfig()\n  const apiKey = config.public.tmdbApiKey\n  const language = config.public.tmdbLanguage || 'en'\n  const baseUrl = 'https://api.themoviedb.org/3'\n  const imageBaseUrl = 'https://image.tmdb.org/t/p'\n\n  // Image sizes\n  const imageSizes = {\n    w185: `${imageBaseUrl}/w185`,\n    w342: `${imageBaseUrl}/w342`,\n    w500: `${imageBaseUrl}/w500`,\n    w780: `${imageBaseUrl}/w780`,\n    original: `${imageBaseUrl}/original`,\n  }\n\n  function getImageUrl(path: string | null, size: 'w185' | 'w342' | 'w500' | 'w780' | 'original' = 'w500'): string | null {\n    if (!path) return null\n    return `${imageSizes[size]}${path}`\n  }\n\n  async function fetchTmdb(endpoint: string, params: Record<string, any> = {}) {\n    const url = new URL(`${baseUrl}${endpoint}`)\n    url.searchParams.set('api_key', apiKey)\n    url.searchParams.set('language', language)\n    \n    Object.entries(params).forEach(([key, value]) => {\n      if (value !== undefined && value !== null) {\n        url.searchParams.set(key, String(value))\n      }\n    })\n\n    try {\n      const response = await $fetch(url.toString())\n      return response\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error('TMDB API Error:', error)\n      throw error\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Trending content\n  async function getTrending(timeWindow: 'day' | 'week' = 'day', page: number = 1): Promise<TmdbTrendingResponse> {\n    return fetchTmdb('/trending/all', { time_window: timeWindow, page })\n  }\n\n  // Movies\n  async function getPopularMovies(page: number = 1) {\n    return fetchTmdb('/movie/popular', { page })\n  }\n\n  async function getTopRatedMovies(page: number = 1) {\n    return fetchTmdb('/movie/top_rated', { page })\n  }\n\n  async function getUpcomingMovies(page: number = 1) {\n    return fetchTmdb('/movie/upcoming', { page })\n  }\n\n  async function getMovieDetails(id: number): Promise<TmdbMovieDetails> {\n ```diff
import type {
  TmdbMovie,
  TmdbTvShow,
  TmdbTrendingResponse,
  TmdbSearchResponse,
  TmdbMovieDetails,
  TmdbTvDetails,
  TmdbSeasonDetails,
  TmdbGenre,
} from '~/types/tmdb'

export function useTmdb() {
  const config = useRuntimeConfig()
  const apiKey = config.public.tmdbApiKey
  const language = config.public.tmdbLanguage || 'en'
  const baseUrl = 'https://api.themoviedb.org/3'
  const imageBaseUrl = 'https://image.tmdb.org/t/p'

  // Image sizes
  const imageSizes = {
    w185: `${imageBaseUrl}/w185`,
    w342: `${imageBaseUrl}/w342`,
    w500: `${imageBaseUrl}/w500`,
    w780: `${imageBaseUrl}/w780`,
    original: `${imageBaseUrl}/original`,
  }

  function getImageUrl(path: string | null, size: 'w185' | 'w342' | 'w500' | 'w780' | 'original' = 'w500'): string | null {
    if (!path) return null
    return `${imageSizes[size]}${path}`
  }

  async function fetchTmdb(endpoint: string, params: Record<string, any> = {}) {
    const url = new URL(`${baseUrl}${endpoint}`)
    url.searchParams.set('api_key', apiKey)
    url.searchParams.set('language', language)
    
    Object.entries(params).forEach(([key, value]) => {
      if (value !== undefined && value !== null) {
        url.searchParams.set(key, String(value))
      }
    })

    try {
      const response = await $fetch(url.toString())
      return response
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('TMDB API Error:', error)
      throw error
    }
  }

  // Trending content
  async function getTrending(timeWindow: 'day' | 'week' = 'day', page: number = 1): Promise<TmdbTrendingResponse> {
    return fetchTmdb('/trending/all', { time_window: timeWindow, page })
  }

  // Movies
  async function getPopularMovies(page: number = 1) {
    return fetchTmdb('/movie/popular', { page })
  }

  async function getTopRatedMovies(page: number = 1) {
    return fetchTmdb('/movie/top_rated', { page })
  }

  async function getUpcomingMovies(page: number = 1) {
    return fetchTmdb('/movie/upcoming', { page })
  }

  async function getMovieDetails(id: number): Promise<TmdbMovieDetails> {
    return fetchTmdb(`/movie/${id}`, {
      append_to_response: 'credits,similar',
    })
  }

  // TV Shows
  async function getPopularTvShows(page: number = 1) {
    return fetchTmdb('/tv/popular', { page })
  }

  async function getTopRatedTvShows(page: number = 1) {
    return fetchTmdb('/tv/top_rated', { page })
  }

  async function getOnTheAirTvShows(page: number = 1) {
    return fetchTmdb('/tv/on_the_air', { page })
  }

  async function getTvDetails(id: number): Promise<TmdbTvDetails> {
    return fetchTmdb(`/tv/${id}`, {
      append_to_response: 'credits,similar',
    })
  }

  async function getSeasonDetails(tmdbId: number, seasonNumber: number): Promise<TmdbSeasonDetails> {
    return fetchTmdb(`/tv/${tmdbId}/season/${seasonNumber}`)
  }

  // Search
  async function searchMulti(query: string, page: number = 1): Promise<TmdbSearchResponse> {
    return fetchTmdb('/search/multi', { query, page })
  }

  async function searchMovies(query: string, page: number = 1) {
    return fetchTmdb('/search/movie', { query, page })
  }

  async function searchTv(query: string, page: number = 1) {
    return fetchTmdb('/search/tv', { query, page })
  }

  // Genres
  async function getMovieGenres(): Promise<TmdbGenre[]> {
    const response = await fetchTmdb('/genre/movie/list')
    return response.genres
  }

  async function getTvGenres(): Promise<TmdbGenre[]> {
    const response = await fetchTmdb('/genre/tv/list')
    return response.genres
  }

  // Recommendations
  async function getMovieRecommendations(id: number, page: number = 1) {
    return fetchTmdb(`/movie/${id}/recommendations`, { page })
  }

  async function getTvRecommendations(id: number, page: number = 1) {
    return fetchTmdb(`/tv/${id}/recommendations`, { page })
  }

  return {
    getImageUrl,
    getTrending,
    getPopularMovies,
    getTopRatedMovies,
    getUpcomingMovies,
    getMovieDetails,
    getPopularTvShows,
    getTopRatedTvShows,
    getOnTheAirTvShows,
    getTvDetails,
    getSeasonDetails,
    searchMulti,
    searchMovies,
    searchTv,
    getMovieGenres,
    getTvGenres,
    getMovieRecommendations,
    getTvRecommendations,
  }
}

```diff
export function useVidSrc() {
  const config = useRuntimeConfig()
  const baseUrl = config.public.vidsrcBaseUrl || 'https://vidsrc-embed.ru'

  // Movie embed URL
  function getMovieEmbedUrl(
    tmdbId: number,
    options: {
      ds_lang?: string
      sub_url?: string
      autoplay?: boolean
    } = {}
  ): string {
    const url = new URL(`${baseUrl}/embed/movie`)
    url.searchParams.set('tmdb', String(tmdbId))
    
    if (options.ds_lang) {
      url.searchParams.set('ds_lang', options.ds_lang)
    }
    
    if (options.sub_url) {
      url.searchParams.set('sub_url', options.sub_url)
    }
    
    if (options.autoplay !== undefined) {
      url.searchParams.set('autoplay', options.autoplay ? '1' : '0')
    }

    return url.toString()
  }

  // TV episode embed URL
  function getTvEmbedUrl(
    tmdbId: number,
    season: number,
    episode: number,
    options: {
      ds_lang?: string
      sub_url?: string
      autoplay?: boolean
      autonext?: boolean
    } = {}
  ): string {
    const url = new URL(`${baseUrl}/embed/tv`)
    url.searchParams.set('tmdb', String(tmdbId))
    url.searchParams.set('season', String(season))
    url.searchParams.set('episode', String(episode))
    
    if (options.ds_lang) {
      url.searchParams.set('ds_lang', options.ds_lang)
    }
    
    if (options.sub_url) {
      url.searchParams.set('sub_url', options.sub_url)
    }
    
    if (options.autoplay !== undefined) {
      url.searchParams.set('autoplay', options.autoplay ? '1' : '0')
    }
    
    if (options.autonext !== undefined) {
      url.searchParams.set('autonext', options.autonext ? '1' : '0')
    }

    return url.toString()
  }

  // Latest movies from vidsrc
  async function getLatestMovies(page: number = 1) {
    try {
      const response = await $fetch(`${baseUrl}/movies/latest/page-${page}.json`)
      return response
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching latest movies:', error)
      return { results: [] }
    }
  }

  // Latest TV shows from vidsrc
  async function getLatestTvShows(page: number = 1) {
    try {
      const response = await $fetch(`${baseUrl}/tvshows/latest/page-${page}.json`)
      return response
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching latest TV shows:', error)
      return { results: [] }
    }
  }

  // Latest episodes from vidsrc
  async function getLatestEpisodes(page: number = 1) {
    try {
      const response = await $fetch(`${baseUrl}/episodes/latest/page-${page}.json`)
      return response
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching latest episodes:', error)
      return { results: [] }
    }
  }

  return {
    getMovieEmbedUrl,
    getTvEmbedUrl,
    getLatestMovies,
    getLatestTvShows,
    getLatestEpisodes,
  }
}

```diff
import type { WatchHistory, WatchHistoryInput, WatchProgressUpdate } from '~/types/supabase'

export function useWatchHistory() {
  const supabase = useSupabase()
  const { isAuthenticated, currentUser } = useAuth()

  // Get user's watch history
  async function getWatchHistory(limit: number = 50): Promise<WatchHistory[]> {
    if (!isAuthenticated.value || !currentUser.value) {
      return []
    }

    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('watch_history')
        .select('*')
        .eq('user_id', currentUser.value.id)
        .order('last_watched_at', { ascending: false })
        .limit(limit)

      if (error) throw error
      return data || []
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching watch history:', error)
      return []
    }
  }

  // Get continue watching (items with progress > 0)
  async function getContinueWatching(limit: number = 20): Promise<WatchHistory[]> {
    if (!isAuthenticated.value || !currentUser.value) {
      return []
    }

    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('watch_history')
        .select('*')
        .eq('user_id', currentUser.value.id)
        .gt('percentage_complete', 0)
        .lt('percentage_complete', 100)
        .order('last_watched_at', { ascending: false })
        .limit(limit)

      if (error) throw error
      return data || []
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching continue watching:', error)
      return []
    }
  }

  // Start watching (create or update history entry)
  async function startWatching(input: WatchHistoryInput): Promise<void> {
    if (!isAuthenticated.value || !currentUser.value) {
      return
    }

    try {
      const { error } = await supabase
        .from('watch_history')
        .upsert({
          user_id: currentUser.value.id,
          tmdb_id: input.tmdb_id,
          media_type: input.media_type,
          title: input.title,
          poster_url: input.poster_url,
          backdrop_url: input.backdrop_url,
          progress_seconds: input.progress_seconds,
          total_seconds: input.total_seconds,
          percentage_complete: input.percentage_complete,
          season_number: input.season_number || null,
          episode_number: input.episode_number || null,
          last_watched_at: new Date().toISOString(),
        }, {
          onConflict: 'user_id, tmdb_id, season_number, episode_number',
        })

      if (error) throw error
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error starting watch history:', error)
    }
  }

  // Update watch progress
  async function updateProgress(
    tmdbId: number,
    progress: WatchProgressUpdate,
    seasonNumber?: number | null,
    episodeNumber?: number | null
  ): Promise<void> {
    if (!isAuthenticated.value || !currentUser.value) {
      return
    }

    try {
      const { error } = await supabase
        .from('watch_history')
        .update({
          progress_seconds: progress.progress_seconds,
          total_seconds: progress.total_seconds,
          percentage_complete: progress.percentage_complete,
          last_watched_at: new Date().toISOString(),
        })
        .eq('user_id', currentUser.value.id)
        .eq('tmdb_id', tmdbId)
        .is('season_number', seasonNumber || null)
        .is('episode_number', episodeNumber || null)

      if (error) throw error
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error updating watch progress:', error)
    }
  }

  // Get resume point for a specific item
  async function getResumePoint(
    tmdbId: number,
    seasonNumber?: number | null,
    episodeNumber?: number | null
  ): Promise<WatchHistory | null> {
    if (!isAuthenticated.value || !currentUser.value) {
      return null
    }

    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('watch_history')
        .select('*')
        .eq('user_id', currentUser.value.id)
        .eq('tmdb_id', tmdbId)
        .is('season_number', seasonNumber || null)
        .is('episode_number', episodeNumber || null)
        .single()

      if (error) throw error
      return data
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching resume point:', error)
      return null
    }
  }

  // Delete watch history entry
  async function deleteEntry(
    tmdbId: number,
    seasonNumber?: number | null,
    episodeNumber?: number | null
  ): Promise<void> {
    if (!isAuthenticated.value || !currentUser.value) {
      return
    }

    try {
      const { error } = await supabase
        .from('watch_history')
        .delete()
        .eq('user_id', currentUser.value.id)
        .eq('tmdb_id', tmdbId)
        .is('season_number', seasonNumber || null)
        .is('episode_number', episodeNumber || null)

      if (error) throw error
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error deleting watch history:', error)
    }
  }

  // Clear all watch history
  async function clearAllHistory(): Promise<void> {
    if (!isAuthenticated.value || !currentUser.value) {
      return
    }

    try {
      const { error } = await supabase
        .from('watch_history')
        .delete()
        .eq('user_id', currentUser.value.id)

      if (error) throw error
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error clearing watch history:', error)
    }
  }

  return {
    getWatchHistory,
    getContinueWatching,
    startWatching,
    updateProgress,
    getResumePoint,
    deleteEntry,
    clearAllHistory,
  }
}

```

**Output:**

*Diff for `useFocusManager.ts`:*
```diff
export function useFocusManager() {
  const focusedElement = ref<HTMLElement | null>(null)
  const focusRing = ref<HTMLElement[]>([])

  // Get all focusable elements in a container
  function getFocusableElements(container?: HTMLElement): HTMLElement[] {
    const root = container || document
    const selector = 'button, [href], input, select, textarea, [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])'
    return Array.from(root.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(selector))
      .filter(el => !el.hasAttribute('disabled') && el.offsetParent !== null)
  }

  // Set focus to an element
  function focus(element: HTMLElement | null, options?: FocusOptions): void {
    if (!element) return
    element.focus(options)
    focusedElement.value = element
  }

  // Focus first element in container
  function focusFirst(container?: HTMLElement): void {
    const elements = getFocusableElements(container)
    if (elements.length > 0) {
      focus(elements[0])
    }
  }

  // Focus last element in container
  function focusLast(container?: HTMLElement): void {
    const elements = getFocusableElements(container)
    if (elements.length > 0) {
      focus(elements[elements.length - 1])
    }
  }

  // Focus next element in direction
  function focusNext(direction: 'up' | 'down' | 'left' | 'right'): void {
    const current = document.activeElement
    if (!current) return

    const container = current.closest('.focus-container') as HTMLElement | null
    const elements = getFocusableElements(container)
    const currentIndex = elements.indexOf(current as HTMLElement)

    if (currentIndex === -1) return

    let nextIndex: number

    switch (direction) {
      case 'up':
        nextIndex = Math.max(0, currentIndex - 1)
        break
      case 'down':
        nextIndex = Math.min(elements.length - 1, currentIndex + 1)
        break
      case 'left':
        nextIndex = Math.max(0, currentIndex - 1)
        break
      case 'right':
        nextIndex = Math.min(elements.length - 1, currentIndex + 1)
        break
      default:
        return
    }

    focus(elements[nextIndex])
  }

  // Handle keyboard navigation for Android TV
  function handleKeydown(event: KeyboardEvent): void {
    const key = event.key

    // D-pad navigation
    switch (key) {
      case 'ArrowUp':
        event.preventDefault()
        focusNext('up')
        break
      case 'ArrowDown':
        event.preventDefault()
        focusNext('down')
        break
      case 'ArrowLeft':
        event.preventDefault()
        focusNext('left')
        break
      case 'ArrowRight':
        event.preventDefault()
        focusNext('right')
        break
      case 'Enter':
      case ' ':
        if (document.activeElement?.tagName === 'BUTTON' || 
            document.activeElement?.getAttribute('role') === 'button') {
          event.preventDefault()
          ;(document.activeElement as HTMLElement)?.click()
        }
        break
      case 'Backspace':
      case 'Escape':
      case 'Back': // Android TV back button
        event.preventDefault()
        handleBack()
        break
    }
  }

  // Handle back navigation
  function handleBack(): void {
    const router = useRouter()
    router.back()
  }

  // Setup keyboard listener
  onMounted(() => {
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeydown)
  })

  onUnmounted(() => {
    window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeydown)
  })

  return {
    focusedElement,
    focus,
    focusFirst,
    focusLast,
    focusNext,
    getFocusableElements,
    handleBack,
  }
}

```

```diff
-- Pichisfin Database Schema

-- Enable UUID extension
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "uuid-ossp";

-- Profiles table (extends auth.users)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS profiles (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  email TEXT,
  display_name TEXT,
  avatar_url TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Watch history table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS watch_history (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id UUID REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE NOT NULL,
  tmdb_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
  media_type TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (media_type IN ('movie', 'tv')),
  title TEXT NOT NULL,
  poster_url TEXT,
  backdrop_url TEXT,
  progress_seconds INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  total_seconds INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  percentage_complete REAL DEFAULT 0,
  season_number INTEGER,
  episode_number INTEGER,
  last_watched_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  UNIQUE(user_id, tmdb_id, season_number, episode_number)
);

-- Create indexes for fast queries
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_watch_history_user ON watch_history(user_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_watch_history_tmdb ON watch_history(tmdb_id, media_type);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_watch_history_last_watched ON watch_history(last_watched_at DESC);

-- Row Level Security policies
ALTER TABLE profiles ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE watch_history ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Profiles policies
CREATE POLICY "Users can view own profile"
  ON profiles FOR SELECT
  USING (auth.uid() = id);

CREATE POLICY "Users can update own profile"
  ON profiles FOR UPDATE
  USING (auth.uid() = id);

CREATE POLICY "Users can insert own profile"
  ON profiles FOR INSERT
  WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = id);

-- Watch history policies
CREATE POLICY "Users can view own watch history"
  ON watch_history FOR SELECT
  USING (auth.uid() = user_id);

CREATE POLICY "Users can insert own watch history"
  ON watch_history FOR INSERT
  WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

CREATE POLICY "Users can update own watch history"
  ON watch_history FOR UPDATE
  USING (auth.uid() = user_id);

CREATE POLICY "Users can delete own watch history"
  ON watch_history FOR DELETE
  USING (auth.uid() = user_id);

-- Function to handle new user profile creation
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION handle_new_user()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO public.profiles (id, email, display_name)
  VALUES (NEW.id, NEW.email, NEW.raw_user_meta_data->>'display_name');
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;

-- Trigger for new user creation
CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER on_auth_user_created
  AFTER INSERT ON auth.users
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION handle_new_user();

-- Function to update updated_at timestamp
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_updated_at_column()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
  NEW.updated_at = NOW();
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Trigger for updating updated_at
CREATE TRIGGER update_profiles_updated_at
  BEFORE UPDATE ON profiles
  FOR EACH ROW
  EXECUTE FUNCTION update_updated_at_column();

```

  <div class="loading-spinner" :class="[sizeClass, centerClass]">
    <div class="spinner"></div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
const props = defineProps<{
  size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg'
  center?: boolean
}>()

const sizeClass = computed(() => {
  switch (props.size) {
    case 'sm': return 'w-6 h-6'
    case 'lg': return 'w-12 h-12'
    default: return 'w-8 h-8'
  }
})

const centerClass = computed(() => props.center ? 'flex justify-center items-center' : '')
</script>

<template>

      v-if="!error"

defineAttrs()

    case 'profile': return 'aspect-square'
    case 'thumbnail': return 'aspect-[16/9]'

<script setup lang="ts">
const props = defineProps<{
  title: string
  tmdbId: number
  posterPath: string | null
  mediaType?: 'movie' | 'tv'
  imageSize?: 'poster' | 'backdrop' | 'thumbnail'
  rating?: number
  year?: number
  genres?: string[]
  progress?: number
  showProgress?: boolean
  showRating?: boolean
  showOverlay?: boolean
}>()

const tmdb = useTmdb()

const posterUrl = computed(() => {
  if (!props.posterPath) return null
  return tmdb.getImageUrl(props.posterPath, props.imageSize === 'backdrop' ? 'w780' : 'w500')
})

const link = computed(() => {
  const type = props.mediaType || 'movie'
  return `/${type}-${props.tmdbId}`
})

function onFocus() {
  // Could add sound effect or animation here
}

function onBlur() {
  // Could add cleanup here
}
</script>

}

  @apply relative overflow-hidden rounded-md;
}

}

.tv-card:hover .card-overlay,

}

}
</style>

```diff
<template>
  <section v-if="items.length > 0" class="tv-carousel">
    <div class="carousel-header">
      <h2 class="carousel-title">{{ title }}</h2>
      <NuxtLink v-if="showViewAll" :to="viewAllLink" class="view-all-link">
        View All
      </NuxtLink>
    </div>
    
    <div class="carousel-content" ref="carouselRef">
      <button
        v-if="showLeftArrow"
        class="carousel-btn carousel-btn-left"
        @click="scrollLeft"
        @keydown.enter="scrollLeft"
        tabindex="0"
        aria-label="Scroll left"
      >
        <svg class="w-6 h-6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M15 19l-7-7 7-7" />
        </svg>
      </button>
      
      <div class="carousel-track" ref="trackRef">
        <slot :items="items"></slot>
      </div>
      
      <button
        v-if="showRightArrow"
        class="carousel-btn carousel-btn-right"
        @click="scrollRight"
        @keydown.enter="scrollRight"
        tabindex="0"
        aria-label="Scroll right"
      >
        <svg class="w-6 h-6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9 5l7 7-7 7" />
        </svg>
      </button>
    </div>
  </section>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
defineProps<{
  title: string
  showViewAll?: boolean
  viewAllLink?: string
}>()

const carouselRef = ref<HTMLElement | null>(null)
const trackRef = ref<HTMLElement | null>(null)

const showLeftArrow = ref(false)
const showRightArrow = ref(true)

const items = ref<any[]>([])

function updateArrowVisibility() {
  if (!trackRef.value) return
  
  const { scrollLeft, scrollWidth, clientWidth } = trackRef.value
  showLeftArrow.value = scrollLeft > 0
  showRightArrow.value = scrollLeft < scrollWidth - clientWidth - 10
}

function scrollLeft() {
  if (trackRef.value) {
    trackRef.value.scrollBy({ left: -400, behavior: 'smooth' })
  }
}

function scrollRight() {
  if (trackRef.value) {
    trackRef.value.scrollBy({ left: 400, behavior: 'smooth' })
  }
}

onMounted(() => {
  if (trackRef.value) {
    trackRef.value.addEventListener('scroll', updateArrowVisibility)
  }
})

onUnmounted(() => {
  if (trackRef.value) {
    trackRef.value.removeEventListener('scroll', updateArrowVisibility)
  }
})
</script>

<style scoped>
.tv-carousel {
  @apply py-6;
}

.carousel-header {
  @apply flex justify-between items-center px-4 md:px-8 mb-4;
}

.carousel-title {
  @apply text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold text-white;
}

.view-all-link {
  @apply text-netflix-lightGray text-sm hover:text-white transition-colors;
}

.carousel-content {
  @apply relative;
}

.carousel-track {
  @apply flex gap-3 overflow-x-auto carousel-container;
  padding: 0 1rem;
  scroll-behavior: smooth;
  -webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch;
}

.carousel-btn {
  @apply absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 z-10;
  @apply bg-netflix-dark bg-opacity-80 hover:bg-opacity-100;
  @apply text-white p-2 rounded-full transition-all duration-200;
  @apply focus:ring-4 focus:ring-white focus:ring-opacity-50;
}

.carousel-btn-left {
  @apply left-0 -translate-x-1/2;
}

.carousel-btn-right {
  @apply right-0 translate-x-1/2;
}

.carousel-btn:hover {
  transform: translateY(-50%) scale(1.1);
}
</style>

```

@click=\"loadEpisodes\">\n        Retry\n      </button>\n    </div>\n    \n    <div v-else class=\"episodes-grid\">\n      <button\n        v-for=\"episode in episodes\"\n        :key=\"episode.id\"\n        class=\"episode-card episode-card-focus\"\n        :class=\"{ 'episode-playing': isPlaying(episode) }\"\n        @click=\"selectEpisode(episode)\"\n        @keydown.enter=\"selectEpisode(episode)\"\n        tabindex=\"0\"\n      >\n        <div class=\"episode-number\">{{ episode.episode_number }}</div>\n        <div class=\"episode-info\">\n <script setup lang="ts">
const { signIn, signInWithGoogle } = useAuth()
const router = useRouter()

const email = ref('')
const password = ref('')
const loading = ref(false)
const error = ref('')

async function handleSignIn() {
  loading.value = true
  error.value = ''
  
  const result = await signIn(email.value, password.value)
  
  if (result.success) {
    await router.push('/')
  } else {
    error.value = result.error || 'Sign in failed'
  }
  
  loading.value = false
}

async function handleGoogleSignIn() {
  const result = await signInWithGoogle()
  if (!result.success) {
    error.value = result.error || 'Google sign in failed'
  }
}
</script>

<style scoped>

  @apply min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center p-4;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #141414 0%, #1a1a1a 100%);
}

  @apply bg-netflix-dark bg-opacity-80 p-8 rounded-lg;
  @apply max-w-md w-full;
  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
}

  @apply text-3xl font-bold text-white mb-6 text-center;
}

}

}

.form-label {

}

.form-input {
  @apply w-full px-4 py-3 bg-netflix-black border border-netflix-gray;
  @apply rounded text-white placeholder-netflix-gray;
  @apply focus:ring-2 focus:ring-netflix-red focus:border-netflix-red;
  @apply transition-all duration-200;
}

.btn-submit {
  @apply w-full flex items-center justify-center;
  min-height: 48px;
}

.form-divider {
  @apply flex items-center justify-center my-6;
}

.form-divider::before,
.form-divider::after {
  content: '';
  flex: 1;
  border-bottom: 1px solid #333;
}

.form-divider span {
  @apply px-4 text-netflix-gray text-sm;
}

.btn-google {
  @apply w-full flex items-center justify-center;
}

.form-footer {
  @apply text-center text-netflix-lightGray text-sm mt-6;
}

.form-link {
  @apply text-netflix-red hover:text-netflix-darkRed transition-colors;
}
</style>

<template>
  <div class="sign-up-form">
    <form @submit.prevent="handleSignUp" class="form-container">
      <h2 class="form-title">Create Account</h2>
      
      <div v-if="error" class="form-error">
        {{ error }}
      </div>
      
      <div class="form-group">
        <label for="displayName" class="form-label">Display Name</label>
        <input
          id="displayName"
          v-model="displayName"
          type="text"
          class="form-input"
          placeholder="Enter your display name"
          tabindex="0"
        />
      </div>
      
      <div class="form-group">
        <label for="email" class="form-label">Email</label>
        <input
          id="email"
          v-model="email"
          type="email"
          class="form-input"
          placeholder="Enter your email"
          required
          tabindex="0"
        />
      </div>
      
      <div class="form-group">
        <label for="password" class="form-label">Password</label>
        <input
          id="password"
          v-model="password"
          type="password"
          class="form-input"
          placeholder="Create a password (min 6 characters)"
          required
          minlength="6"
          tabindex="0"
        />
      </div>
      
      <div class="form-group">
        <label for="confirmPassword" class="form-label">Confirm Password</label>
        <input
          id="confirmPassword"
          v-model="confirmPassword"
          type="password"
          class="form-input"
          placeholder="Confirm your password"
          required
          tabindex="0"
        />
      </div>
      
      <button
        type="submit"
        class="btn-primary btn-submit"
        :disabled="loading || !isValid"
        tabindex="0"
      >
        <LoadingSpinner v-if="loading" size="sm" />
        <span v-else>Sign Up</span>
      </button>
      
      <p class="form-footer">
        Already have an account?
        <NuxtLink to="/signin" class="form-link">Sign in</NuxtLink>
      </p>
    </form>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
const { signUp } = useAuth()
const router = useRouter()

const displayName = ref('')
const email = ref('')
const password = ref('')
const confirmPassword = ref('')
const loading = ref(false)
const error = ref('')

const isValid = computed(() => {
  return password.value === confirmPassword.value && 
         password.value.length >= 6 && 
         email.value.includes('@')
})

async function handleSignUp() {
  if (!isValid.value) return
  
  loading.value = true
  error.value = ''
  
  const result = await signUp(email.value, password.value, displayName.value || undefined)
  
  if (result.success) {
    await router.push('/')
  } else {
    error.value = result.error || 'Sign up failed'
  }
  
  loading.value = false
}
</script>

<style scoped>
.sign-up-form {
  @apply min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center p-4;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #141414 0%, #1a1a1a 100%);
}

.form-container {
  @apply bg-netflix-dark bg-opacity-80 p-8 rounded-lg;
  @apply max-w-md w-full;
  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
}

<template>
  <div class="video-player" :class="{ fullscreen: isFullscreen }">
    <!-- Loading state -->
    <div v-if="loading" class="player-loading">
      <LoadingSpinner size="lg" center />
      <p class="loading-text">Loading player...</p>
    </div>
    
    <!-- Error state -->
    <div v-else-if="error" class="player-error">
      <svg class="w-16 h-16 text-netflix-red" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
        <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 8v4m0 4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
      </svg>
      <h3 class="error-title">Unable to load video</h3>
      <p class="error-message">{{ errorMessage }}</p>
      <button class="btn-primary mt-4" @click="retry">Retry</button>
    </div>
    
    <!-- Video iframe -->
    <div v-else class="player-container">
      <iframe
        :src="embedUrl"
        class="player-iframe"
        allowfullscreen
        allow="autoplay; fullscreen; picture-in-picture"
        sandbox="allow-scripts allow-same-origin allow-presentation allow-popups"
        @load="onIframeLoad"
        @error="onIframeError"
      ></iframe>
    </div>
    
    <!-- Player controls overlay -->
    <div class="player-controls">
      <button
        class="control-btn"
        @click="goBack"
        tabindex="0"
        aria-label="Go back"
      >
        <svg class="w-6 h-6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M10 19l-7-7m0 0l7-7m-7 7h18" />
        </svg>
      </button>
      
      <div class="control-title">
        <h3>{{ title }}</h3>
        <p v-if="episodeInfo" class="episode-info">{{ episodeInfo }}</p>
      </div>
      
      <button
        class="control-btn"
        @click="toggleFullscreen"
        tabindex="0"
        aria-label="Toggle fullscreen"
      >
        <svg v-if="!isFullscreen" class="w-6 h-6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M4 8V4m0 0h4M4 4l5 5m11-1V4m0 0h-4m4 0l-5 5M4 16v4m0 0h4m-4 0l5-5m11 5l-5-5m5 5v-4m0 4h-4" />
        </svg>
        <svg v-else class="w-6 h-6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9 9V4.5M9 9H4.5M9 9L3.75 3.75M9 15v4.5M9 15H4.5M9 15l-5.25 5.25M15 9h4.5M15 9V4.5M15 9l5.25-5.25M15 15h4.5M15 15v4.5M15 15l5.25 5.25" />
        </svg>
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
const props = defineProps<{
  embedUrl: string
  title: string
  episodeInfo?: string
}>()

const emit = defineEmits<{
  play: []
  pause: []
  progress: [seconds: number]
  back: []
}>()

const loading = ref(true)
const error = ref(false)
const errorMessage = ref('')
const isFullscreen = ref(false)

onMounted(() => {
  emit('play')
})

function onIframeLoad() {
  loading.value = false
}

function onIframeError() {
  loading.value = false
  error.value = true
  errorMessage.value = 'Failed to load video player'
}

function retry() {
  error.value = false
  loading.value = true
  // Force reload
  const currentUrl = props.embedUrl
  nextTick(() => {
    // This will trigger a re-render
  })
}

function goBack() {
  emit('back')
}

function toggleFullscreen() {
  if (!document.fullscreenElement) {
    document.documentElement.requestFullscreen()
    isFullscreen.value = true
  } else {
    document.exitFullscreen()
    isFullscreen.value = false
  }
}

// Listen for fullscreen changes
onMounted(() => {
  document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', () => {
    isFullscreen.value = !!document.fullscreenElement
  })
})

onUnmounted(() => {
  document.removeEventListener('fullscreenchange', () => {})
})
</script>

<style scoped>
.video-player {
  @apply relative w-full;
  aspect-ratio: 16/9;
  background: #000;
}

.video-player.fullscreen {

  height: 100vh;
}

  @apply absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center;
}

}

}

}

}

}

}

.player-controls {
  @apply absolute top-0 left-0 right-0 z-10;

  background: linear-gradient(to bottom, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.7), transparent);
}

<template>
  <div class="episode-selector">
    <h3 class="selector-title">Select Episode</h3>
    
    <div class="episodes-grid">
      <button
        v-for="episode in episodes"
        :key="episode.id"
        class="episode-card episode-card-focus"
        :class="{ 'episode-active': isActive(episode) }"
        @click="selectEpisode(episode)"
        @keydown.enter="selectEpisode(episode)"
        tabindex="0"
      >
        <div class="episode-number">{{ episode.episode_number }}</div>
        <div class="episode-info">
          <h4 class="episode-name">{{ episode.name }}</h4>
          <p v-if="episode.air_date" class="episode-date">
            {{ formatDate(episode.air_date) }}
          </p>
        </div>
        <div v-if="episode.runtime" class="episode-runtime">
          {{ episode.runtime }} min
        </div>
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
import type { TmdbEpisode } from '~/types/tmdb'

const props = defineProps<{
  episodes: TmdbEpisode[]
  currentSeason?: number
  currentEpisode?: number
}>()

const emit = defineEmits<{
  select: [episode: TmdbEpisode]
}>()

function isActive(episode: TmdbEpisode): boolean {
  return props.currentSeason === episode.season_number && 
         props.currentEpisode === episode.episode_number
}

function selectEpisode(episode: TmdbEpisode) {
  emit('select', episode)
}

function formatDate(dateString: string): string {
  const date = new Date(dateString)
  return date.toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
    month: 'short',
    day: 'numeric',
    year: 'numeric',
  })
}
</script>

<style scoped>
.episode-selector {
  @apply p-4;
}

.selector-title {
  @apply text-xl font-bold text-white mb-4;
}

.episodes-grid {
  @apply grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3;
}

.episode-card {
  @apply bg-netflix-dark hover:bg-netflix-gray transition-colors;
  @apply p-4 rounded cursor-pointer text-left;
  @apply focus:ring-2 focus:ring-white;
}

.episode-active {
  @apply bg-netflix-red bg-opacity-30 ring-2 ring-netflix-red;
}

.episode-number {
  @apply text-2xl font-bold text-netflix-red mb-2;
}

.episode-name {
  @apply text-white text-sm font-semibold mb-1;
}

.episode-date {
  @apply text-netflix-lightGray text-xs;
}

.episode-runtime {
  @apply text-netflix-gray text-xs mt-2;
}
</style>

          />

            class="clear-btn"

<script setup lang="ts">
import type { TmdbSearchResult } from '~/types/tmdb'

const props = defineProps<{
  isOpen: boolean
}>()

const emit = defineEmits<{
  close: []
  select: [item: TmdbSearchResult]
}>()

const tmdb = useTmdb()

const query = ref('')
const results = ref<TmdbSearchResult[]>([])
const loading = ref(false)
const hasSearched = ref(false)
const searchInputRef = ref<HTMLInputElement | null>(null)

let searchTimeout: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null

function open() {
  query.value = ''
  results.value = []
  hasSearched.value = false
  
  nextTick(() => {
    searchInputRef.value?.focus()
  })
}

function close() {
  emit('close')
}

function onEnter() {
  if (results.value.length > 0) {
    emit('select', results.value[0])
    close()
  }
}

async function handleSearch() {
  if (searchTimeout) {
    clearTimeout(searchTimeout)
  }
  
  if (!query.value || query.value.length < 2) {
    results.value = []
    hasSearched.value = false
    return
  }
  
  searchTimeout = setTimeout(async () => {
    loading.value = true
    
    try {
      const response = await tmdb.searchMulti(query.value)
      results.value = response.results.filter(
        item => item.media_type === 'movie' || item.media_type === 'tv'
      )
      hasSearched.value = true
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Search error:', error)
    } finally {
      loading.value = false
    }
  }, 300)
}

function clearSearch() {
  query.value = ''
  results.value = []
  hasSearched.value = false
  searchInputRef.value?.focus()
}

function getPosterUrl(path: string | null): string | null {
  if (!path) return null
  return tmdb.getImageUrl(path, 'w500')
}

function getItemTitle(item: TmdbSearchResult): string {
  return item.title || item.name || 'Unknown'
}

function getItemYear(item: TmdbSearchResult): string | null {
  const date = item.release_date || item.first_air_date
  if (!date) return null
  return date.split('-')[0]
}

function getItemLink(item: TmdbSearchResult): string {
  return `/${item.media_type}-${item.id}`
}

// Expose methods for parent component
defineExpose({
  open,
})
</script>

<style scoped>
.search-overlay {
  @apply fixed inset-0 z-50;
}

.search-backdrop {
  @apply absolute inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-75;
}

.search-container {
  @apply relative z-10;
  @apply absolute top-0 left-0 right-0;
  max-height: 80vh;
  overflow-y: auto;
  background: #1a1a1a;
}

.search-header {
  @apply sticky top-0 z-20;
  @apply flex items-center gap-4 p-4;
  background: #1a1a1a;
  border-bottom: 1px solid #333;
}

.search-input-container {
  @apply flex-1 flex items-center;
  @apply bg-netflix-black border border-netflix-gray rounded;
  @apply px-4 py-3;
}

.search-input-container:focus-within {
  border-color: #fff;
}

.search-icon {
  @apply w-5 h-5 text-netflix-gray mr-3;
}

.search-input {
  @apply flex-1 bg-transparent text-white;
  @apply focus:outline-none;
}

.clear-btn {
  @apply text-netflix-gray hover:text-white transition-colors;
}

.close-btn {
  @apply text-white p-2;
}

.search-loading,
.search-empty,
.search-initial {
  @apply flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16;
}

.search-results {
  @apply p-4;
}

.results-header {
  @apply flex justify-between items-center mb-4;
}

.results-title {
  @apply text-xl font-bold text-white;
}

.results-count {
  @apply text-netflix-lightGray text-sm;
}

.results-grid {
  @apply grid grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5 xl:grid-cols-6 gap-4;
}

.result-card {
  @apply block cursor-pointer;
  transition: transform 0.2s;
}

.result-card:hover,
.result-card:focus {
  transform: scale(1.05);
}

.result-info {
  @apply mt-2;
}

.result-title {
  @apply text-white text-sm font-semibold truncate;
}

.result-meta {
  @apply flex items-center gap-2 mt-1;
}

.result-type {
  @apply text-netflix-lightGray text-xs uppercase;
}

.result-year {
  @apply text-netflix-gray text-xs;
}

.result-rating {
  @apply text-yellow-500 text-xs;
}

.empty-title {
  @apply text-white text-xl font-bold mt-4;
}

.empty-text,
.initial-text {
  @apply text-netflix-lightGray mt-2;
}
</style>

<script setup lang="ts">
const { isAuthenticated, currentUser, signOut, checkSession } = useAuth()
const route = useRoute()

const isScrolled = ref(false)
const isSearchOpen = ref(false)
const searchOverlayRef = ref<any>(null)

onMounted(async () => {
  await checkSession()
  
  window.addEventListener('scroll', handleScroll)
})

onUnmounted(() => {
  window.removeEventListener('scroll', handleScroll)
})

const userName = computed(() => {
  if (!currentUser.value) return ''
  return currentUser.value.user_metadata?.display_name || 
         currentUser.value.email?.split('@')[0] || 
         'User'
})

function handleScroll() {
  isScrolled.value = window.scrollY > 50
}

function isActive(path: string): boolean {
  return route.path === path
}

function toggleSearch() {
  if (isSearchOpen.value) {
    closeSearch()
  } else {
    isSearchOpen.value = true
    nextTick(() => {
      searchOverlayRef.value?.open()
    })
  }
}

function closeSearch() {
  isSearchOpen.value = false
}

async function handleSignOut() {
  await signOut()
}
</script>

<style scoped>

}

  @apply text-white font-semibold;

}

.nav-icon-btn {

export default defineNuxtRouteMiddleware((to) => {
  const { isAuthenticated, loading } = useAuth()
  
  // Wait for auth state to be loaded
  if (loading.value) {
    return abortNavigation()
  }
  
  // If not authenticated and trying to access protected route
  if (!isAuthenticated.value && to.path !== '/signin' && to.path !== '/signup') {
    return navigateTo('/signin')
  }
})

```

<script setup lang="ts">
const tmdb = useTmdb()
const { isAuthenticated } = useAuth()
const { getContinueWatching } = useWatchHistory()
const router = useRouter()

const loading = ref(true)

// Data stores
const trending = ref<any[]>([])
const popularMovies = ref<any[]>([])
const popularTv = ref<any[]>([])
const topRatedMovies = ref<any[]>([])
const topRatedTv = ref<any[]>([])
const continueWatching = ref<any[]>([])

// Featured content (random from trending)
const featuredContent = ref(false)
const featuredTitle = ref('')
const featuredOverview = ref('')
const featuredBackdrop = ref<string | null>(null)
const featuredRating = ref<number | null>(null)
const featuredYear = ref<number | null>(null)
const featuredMediaType = ref<'movie' | 'tv'>('movie')
const featuredId = ref<number>(0)

async function fetchHomeData() {
  try {
    // Fetch all data in parallel
    const [
      trendingResponse,
      popularMoviesResponse,
      popularTvResponse,
      topRatedMoviesResponse,
      topRatedTvResponse,
    ] = await Promise.all([
      tmdb.getTrending('week'),
      tmdb.getPopularMovies(),
      tmdb.getPopularTvShows(),
      tmdb.getTopRatedMovies(),
      tmdb.getTopRatedTvShows(),
    ])

    trending.value = trendingResponse.results
    popularMovies.value = popularMoviesResponse.results
    popularTv.value = popularTvResponse.results
    topRatedMovies.value = topRatedMoviesResponse.results
    topRatedTv.value = topRatedTvResponse.results

    // Set featured content (random from trending)
    if (trending.value.length > 0) {
      const randomIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * trending.value.length)
      const featured = trending.value[randomIndex]
      
      featuredContent.value = true
      featuredTitle.value = featured.title || featured.name || ''
      featuredOverview.value = featured.overview
      featuredBackdrop.value = featured.backdrop_path
      featuredRating.value = featured.vote_average
      featuredYear.value = getYearFromRelease(featured)
      featuredMediaType.value = featured.media_type || 'movie'
      featuredId.value = featured.id
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error fetching home data:', error)
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

async function fetchContinueWatching() {
  if (!isAuthenticated.value) return
  
  try {
    const history = await getContinueWatching()
    continueWatching.value = history
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error fetching continue watching:', error)
  }
}

function getMediaType(item: any): 'movie' | 'tv' {
  return item.media_type || 'movie'
}

function getMediaTitle(item: any): string {
  return item.title || item.name || 'Unknown'
}

function getYearFromRelease(item: any): number | null {
  const date = item.release_date || item.first_air_date
  if (!date) return null
  return parseInt(date.split('-')[0])
}

function handleFeaturedPlay() {
  const type = featuredMediaType.value
  router.push({
    path: '/watch',
    query: {
      tmdb: String(featuredId.value),
      type,
      title: featuredTitle.value,
    },
  })
}

function handleFeaturedInfo() {
  const type = featuredMediaType.value
  router.push(`/${type}-${featuredId.value}`)
}

// Fetch data on mount
onMounted(async () => {
  await Promise.all([
    fetchHomeData(),
    fetchContinueWatching(),
  ])
})
</script>

<style scoped>
.home-page {
  @apply min-h-screen;
}

.page-loading {
  @apply min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center;
}

.content-sections {
  @apply relative z-10 -mt-32;
  padding-bottom: 2rem;
}

.carousel-item {
  @apply flex-shrink-0 w-40 md:w-48 lg:w-56;
}
</style>

<script setup lang="ts">
import type { TmdbSearchResult } from '~/types/tmdb'

const tmdb = useTmdb()
const route = useRoute()

const query = ref(route.query.q as string || '')
const results = ref<TmdbSearchResult[]>([])
const loading = ref(false)
const hasSearched = ref(false)

let searchTimeout: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null

onMounted(async () => {
  if (query.value) {
    await performSearch()
  }
})

async function handleSearch() {
  if (searchTimeout) {
    clearTimeout(searchTimeout)
  }
  
  if (!query.value || query.value.length < 2) {
    results.value = []
    hasSearched.value = false
    return
  }
  
  searchTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
    performSearch()
  }, 300)
}

async function performSearch() {
  loading.value = true
  
  try {
    const response = await tmdb.searchMulti(query.value)
    results.value = response.results.filter(
      item => item.media_type === 'movie' || item.media_type === 'tv'
    )
    hasSearched.value = true
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Search error:', error)
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

function getPosterUrl(path: string | null): string | null {
  if (!path) return null
  return tmdb.getImageUrl(path, 'w500')
}

function getItemTitle(item: TmdbSearchResult): string {
  return item.title || item.name || 'Unknown'
}

function getItemYear(item: TmdbSearchResult): string | null {
  const date = item.release_date || item.first_air_date
  if (!date) return null
  return date.split('-')[0]
}

function getItemLink(item: TmdbSearchResult): string {
  return `/${item.media_type}-${item.id}`
}
</script>

<style scoped>
.search-page {
  @apply max-w-screen-xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-8 py-8;
}

.search-header {
  @apply mb-8;
}

.page-title {
  @apply text-3xl font-bold text-white mb-4;
}

.search-input-container {
  @apply flex items-center;
  @apply bg-netflix-dark border border-netflix-gray rounded;
  @apply px-4 py-3;
}

.search-input-container:focus-within {
  border-color: #fff;
}

.search-icon {
  @apply w-5 h-5 text-netflix-gray mr-3;
}

.search-input {
  @apply flex-1 bg-transparent text-white;
  @apply focus:outline-none;
}

.search-loading {
  @apply min-h-[50vh] flex items-center justify-center;
}

.search-results {
  @apply mt-8;
}

.results-header {
  @apply flex justify-between items-center mb-6;
}

.results-title {
  @apply text-2xl font-bold text-white;
}

.results-count {
  @apply text-netflix-lightGray;
}

.results-grid {
  @apply grid grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5 xl:grid-cols-6 gap-4;
}

.result-card {
  @apply block cursor-pointer;
  transition: transform 0.2s;
}

.result-card:hover,
.result-card:focus {
  transform: scale(1.05);
}

.result-info {
  @apply mt-2;
}

.result-title {
  @apply text-white text-sm font-semibold truncate;
}

.result-meta {
  @apply flex items-center gap-2 mt-1;
}

.result-type {
  @apply text-netflix-lightGray text-xs uppercase;
}

.result-year {
  @apply text-netflix-gray text-xs;
}

.result-rating {
  @apply text-yellow-500 text-xs;
}

.search-empty,
.search-initial {
  @apply min-h-[50vh] flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center;
}

.empty-title,
.initial-title {
  @apply text-white text-2xl font-bold mt-4;
}

.empty-text,
.initial-text {
  @apply text-netflix-lightGray mt-2;
}
</style>

${episodeName.value}`\n  }\n  if (mediaType.value === 'tv') {\n    return `Season ${season.value}, Episode ${episode.value}`\n  }\n  return ''\n})\n\n// Generate embed URL\nconst embedUrl = computed(() => {\n  if (!tmdbId.value) return ''\n  \n  if (mediaType.value === 'movie') {\n    return vidSrc.getMovieEmbedUrl(tmdbId.value, {\n      autoplay: true,\n      ds_lang: 'en',\n    })\n  } else {\n    return vidSrc.getTvEmbedUrl(\n      tmdbId.value,\n      season.value,\n      episode.value,\n      {\n        autoplay: true,\n        autonext: true,\n        ds_lang: 'en',\n      }\n    )\n  }\n})\n\nasync function handlePlay() {\n  if (!tmdbId.value) return\n  \n  // Start tracking watch history\n  await startWatching({\n    tmdb_id: tmdbId"
}
