<script setup lang="ts">
import type { TmdbMovieDetails } from '~/types/tmdb'

const tmdb = useTmdb()
const route = useRoute()
const router = useRouter()

const movieId = computed(() => parseInt(route.params.id as string))
const movie = ref<TmdbMovieDetails | null>(null)
const loading = ref(true)

const backdropUrl = computed(() => {
  if (!movie.value?.backdrop_path) return null
  return tmdb.getImageUrl(movie.value.backdrop_path, 'original')
})

const releaseYear = computed(() => {
  if (!movie.value?.release_date) return null
  return movie.value.release_date.split('-')[0]
})

const runtime = computed(() => movie.value?.runtime || 0)

async function fetchMovie() {
  try {
    loading.value = true
    movie.value = await tmdb.getMovieDetails(movieId.value)
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error fetching movie:', error)
    movie.value = null
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

function formatRuntime(minutes: number): string {
  const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60)
  const mins = minutes % 60
  if (hours === 0) return `${mins}m`
  if (mins === 0) return `${hours}h`
  return `${hours}h ${mins}m`
}

function handlePlay() {
  if (!movie.value) return
  
  router.push({
    path: '/watch',
    query: {
      tmdb: String(movie.value.id),
      type: 'movie',
      title: movie.value.title,
    },
  })
}

onMounted(() => {
  fetchMovie()
})

// SEO metadata
useHead({
  title: () => movie.value ? `${movie.value.title} - Pichisfin` : 'Movie - Pichisfin',
  meta: [
    {
      name: 'description',
      key: 'description',
      value: () => movie.value?.overview || 'Watch movies on Pichisfin',
    },
  ],
})
</script>

    rgba(20, 20, 20, 0) 100%
  );
}

      </svg>

    </div>

</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
import type { TmdbTvDetails } from '~/types/tmdb'

const tmdb = useTmdb()
const route = useRoute()
const router = useRouter()

const tvId = computed(() => parseInt(route.params.id as string))
const tvShow = ref<TmdbTvDetails | null>(null)
const loading = ref(true)

const backdropUrl = computed(() => {
  if (!tvShow.value?.backdrop_path) return null
  return tmdb.getImageUrl(tvShow.value.backdrop_path, 'original')
})

const firstAirYear = computed(() => {
  if (!tvShow.value?.first_air_date) return null
  return tvShow.value.first_air_date.split('-')[0]
})

async function fetchTvShow() {
  try {
    loading.value = true
    tvShow.value = await tmdb.getTvDetails(tvId.value)
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error fetching TV show:', error)
    tvShow.value = null
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

function handlePlay() {
  if (!tvShow.value) return
  
  // Play first episode of first season
  router.push({
    path: '/watch',
    query: {
      tmdb: String(tvShow.value.id),
      type: 'tv',
      title: tvShow.value.name,
      season: '1',
      episode: '1',
    },
  })
}

onMounted(() => {
  fetchTvShow()
})

// SEO metadata
useHead({
  title: () => tvShow.value ? `${tvShow.value.name} - Pichisfin` : 'TV Show - Pichisfin',
  meta: [
    {
      name: 'description',
      key: 'description',
      value: () => tvShow.value?.overview || 'Watch TV shows on Pichisfin',
    },
  ],
})
</script>

<style scoped>

      <LoadingSpinner size="lg" center />

    <template v-else-if="season">
      <div class="season-header">
        <NuxtLink
          :to="`/tv-${tvId}`"
          class="back-link"
          tabindex="0"
        >
          <svg class="w-5 h-5 mr-2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
            <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M10 19l-7-7m0 0l7-7m-7 7h18" />
          </svg>
          Back to {{ tvShowName }}
        </NuxtLink>
        
        <h1 class="season-title">{{ season.name }}</h1>
        <p v-if="season.overview" class="season-overview">{{ season.overview }}</p>
      </div>
      
      <div class="episodes-list">
        <div
          v-for="episode in season.episodes"
          :key="episode.id"
          class="episode-card"
        >
          <div class="episode-poster">
            <ImageWithFallback
              :src="episode.still_path ? tmdb.getImageUrl(episode.still_path, 'w500') : null"
              :alt="episode.name"
              size="backdrop"
            />

          </div>

            <div class="episode-header">
              <h2 class="episode-title">
                E{{ episode.episode_number }}: {{ episode.name }}
              </h2>

                {{ episode.runtime }} min
              </span>
            </div>

              {{ formatDate(episode.air_date) }}
            </p>
            
            <p v-if="episode.overview" class="episode-overview">
              {{ episode.overview }}
            </p>
            
            <div class="episode-actions">
              <button
                class="btn-primary btn-play-sm"
                @click="playEpisode(episode)"
                tabindex="0"
              >
                <svg class="w-5 h-5 mr-2" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 20 20">
                  <path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M10 18a8 8 0 100-16 8 8 0 000 16zM9.555 7.168A1 1 0 008 8v4a1 1 0 001.555.832l3-2a1 1 0 000-1.664l-3-2z" clip-rule="evenodd" />
                </svg>
                Play
              </button>
              <span v-if="episode.vote_average" class="episode-rating">
                ⭐ {{ episode.vote_average.toFixed(1) }}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </template>
    
    <!-- Error state -->
    <div v-else class="page-error">
      <svg class="w-16 h-16 text-netflix-red" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
        <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 8v4m0 4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
      </svg>
      <h2 class="error-title">Season not found</h2>
      <p class="error-text">The season you're looking for doesn't exist.</p>
      <NuxtLink :to="`/tv-${tvId}`" class="btn-primary mt-4">Back to Show</NuxtLink>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
import type { TmdbSeasonDetails, TmdbEpisode } from '~/types/tmdb'

const tmdb = useTmdb()
const route = useRoute()
const router = useRouter()

const tvId = computed(() => parseInt(route.params.id as string))
const seasonNumber = computed(() => parseInt(route.params.season as string))

const season = ref<TmdbSeasonDetails | null>(null)
const tvShowName = ref('')
const loading = ref(true)

async function fetchSeason() {
  try {
    loading.value = true
    
    // Fetch season details
    season.value = await tmdb.getSeasonDetails(tvId.value, seasonNumber.value)
    
    // Fetch TV show details for name
    const tvDetails = await tmdb.getTvDetails(tvId.value)
    tvShowName.value = tvDetails.name
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error fetching season:', error)
    season.value = null
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

function formatDate(dateString: string): string {
  const date = new Date(dateString)
  return date.toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
    weekday: 'long',
    year: 'numeric',
    month: 'long',
    day: 'numeric',
  })
}

function playEpisode(episode: TmdbEpisode) {
  router.push({
    path: '/watch',
    query: {
      tmdb: String(tvId.value),
      type: 'tv',
      title: tvShowName.value,
      season: String(episode.season_number),
      episode: String(episode.episode_number),
      episodeName: episode.name,
    },
  })
}

onMounted(() => {
  fetchSeason()
})

// SEO metadata
useHead({
  title: () => season.value 
    ? `${tvShowName.value} - ${season.value.name} - Pichisfin` 
    : 'Season - Pichisfin',
  meta: [
    {
      name: 'description',
      key: 'description',
      value: () => season.value?.overview || `Watch ${tvShowName.value} on Pichisfin`,
    },
  ],
})
</script>

<style scoped>
.season-page {
  @apply max-w-screen-xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-8 py-8;
}

.page-loading {
  @apply min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center;
}

.season-header {
  @apply mb-8;
}

.back-link {
  @apply inline-flex items-center text-netflix-lightGray hover:text-white;
  @apply mb-4 transition-colors;
}

.season-title {
  @apply text-3xl font-bold text-white mb-4;
}

.season-overview {
  @apply text-netflix-lightGray text-lg;
}

.episodes-list {
  @apply space-y-4;
}

.episode-card {
  @apply bg-netflix-dark rounded-lg overflow-hidden;
  @apply flex flex-col md:flex-row;
}

.episode-poster {
  @apply relative md:w-64 flex-shrink-0;
}

.episode-number-overlay {
  @apply absolute top-2 left-2 bg-netflix-red text-white;
  @apply px-2 py-1 rounded text-sm font-bold;
}

.episode-details {
  @apply flex-1 p-4;
}

.episode-header {
  @apply flex justify-between items-start mb-2;
}

.episode-title {
  @apply text-white text-lg font-semibold flex-1;
}

.episode-runtime {
  @apply text-netflix-gray text-sm;
}

.episode-date {
  @apply text-netflix-lightGray text-sm mb-2;
}

.episode-overview {
  @apply text-netflix-white text-sm mb-4 line-clamp-3;
}

.episode-actions {
  @apply flex items-center gap-4;
}

.btn-play-sm {
  @apply flex items-center text-sm;
  padding: 0.5rem 1rem;
}

.episode-rating {
  @apply text-yellow-500 text-sm;
}

.page-error {
  @apply min-h-screen flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center p-8;
}

.error-title {
  @apply text-white text-2xl font-bold mt-4;
}

.error-text {
  @apply text-netflix-lightGray mt-2;
}
</style>

**Affected Files:**
- `/home/daniel/GitHub/pichisfin/src/pages/watch.vue`

**Output:**

*Diff for `watch.vue`:*
```diff
<template>
  <div class="watch-page">
    <!-- Loading state -->
    <div v-if="loading" class="page-loading">
      <LoadingSpinner size="lg" center />
      <p class="loading-text">Loading player...</p>
    </div>
    
    <!-- Video Player -->
    <VideoPlayer
      v-else
      :embed-url="embedUrl"
      :title="pageTitle"
      :episode-info="episodeInfo"
      @play="handlePlay"
      @back="handleBack"
    />
  </div>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
const vidSrc = useVidSrc()
const { startWatching, updateProgress } = useWatchHistory()
const route = useRoute()
const router = useRouter()

const loading = ref(true)

// Parse route query params
const tmdbId = computed(() => parseInt(route.query.tmdb as string) || 0)
const mediaType = computed(() => (route.query.type as 'movie' | 'tv') || 'movie')
const title = computed(() => (route.query.title as string) || 'Now Playing')
const season = computed(() => parseInt(route.query.season as string) || 1)
const episode = computed(() => parseInt(route.query.episode as string) || 1)
const episodeName = computed(() => (route.query.episodeName as string) || '')

// Generate embed URL
const embedUrl = computed(() => {
  if (!tmdbId.value) return ''
  
  if (mediaType.value === 'movie') {
    return vidSrc.getMovieEmbedUrl(tmdbId.value, {
      autoplay: true,
    })
  } else {
    return vidSrc.getTvEmbedUrl(tmdbId.value, season.value, episode.value, {
      autoplay: true,
      autonext: true,
    })
  }
})

const pageTitle = computed(() => title.value)

const episodeInfo = computed(() => {
  if (mediaType.value !== 'tv') return ''
  if (episodeName.value) {
    return `S${season.value}:E${episode.value} - ${episodeName.value}`
  }
  return `Season ${season.value}, Episode ${episode.value}`
})

// Track watch progress
let progressInterval: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null
let currentProgress = 0

async function handlePlay() {
  if (!tmdbId.value) return
  
  // Start tracking watch history
  await startWatching({
    tmdb_id: tmdbId.value,
    media_type: mediaType.value,
    title: title.value,
    poster_url: null,
    backdrop_url: null,
    progress_seconds: 0,
    total_seconds: 0,
    percentage_complete: 0,
    season_number: mediaType.value === 'tv' ? season.value : null,
    episode_number: mediaType.value === 'tv' ? episode.value : null,
  })
  
  // Start progress tracking (every 10 seconds)
  progressInterval = setInterval(async () => {
    currentProgress += 10
    
    // Note: In a real app, you'd get actual progress from the video player
    // This is a placeholder that increments every 10 seconds
    // You'd need to use PostMessage API or similar to get actual iframe progress
    
    await updateProgress(
      tmdbId.value,
      {
        progress_seconds: currentProgress,
        total_seconds: 0, // Would need to fetch from API
        percentage_complete: 0, // Would calculate based on total
      },
      mediaType.value === 'tv' ? season.value : null,
      mediaType.value === 'tv' ? episode.value : null
    )
  }, 10000)
}

function handleBack() {
  // Stop progress tracking
  if (progressInterval) {
    clearInterval(progressInterval)
    progressInterval = null
  }
  
  // Save final progress
  if (tmdbId.value) {
    updateProgress(
      tmdbId.value,
      {
        progress_seconds: currentProgress,
        total_seconds: 0,
        percentage_complete: 0,
      },
      mediaType.value === 'tv' ? season.value : null,
      mediaType.value === 'tv' ? episode.value : null
    )
  }
  
  router.back()
}

onMounted(() => {
  // Validate route params
  if (!tmdbId.value) {
    router.push('/')
    return
  }
  
  loading.value = false
})

onUnmounted(() => {
  if (progressInterval) {
    clearInterval(progressInterval)
  }
})

// Page metadata
useHead({
  title: () => `${title.value} - Pichisfin`,
})

// Disable layout for full-screen player
definePageMeta({
  layout: false,
})
</script>

<style scoped>
.watch-page {
  @apply min-h-screen bg-black;
}

.page-loading {
  @apply min-h-screen flex flex-col items-center justify-center;
}

.loading-text {
  @apply text-netflix-lightGray mt-4;
}
</style>

```

</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
import type { WatchHistory } from '~/types/supabase'

const { isAuthenticated } = useAuth()
const { getContinueWatching, getWatchHistory, deleteEntry } = useWatchHistory()
const router = useRouter()

const activeTab = ref<'continue' | 'history'>('continue')
const continueWatching = ref<WatchHistory[]>([])
const watchHistory = ref<WatchHistory[]>([])
const loading = ref(true)

async function fetchData() {
  if (!isAuthenticated.value) {
    router.push('/signin')
    return
  }
  
  loading.value = true
  
  try {
    const [continueData, historyData] = await Promise.all([
      getContinueWatching(),
      getWatchHistory(),
    ])
    
    continueWatching.value = continueData
    watchHistory.value = historyData
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error fetching data:', error)
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

function formatDate(dateString: string): string {
  const date = new Date(dateString)
  const now = new Date()
  const diff = now.getTime() - date.getTime()
  const days = Math.floor(diff / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24))
  
  if (days === 0) return 'Today'
  if (days === 1) return 'Yesterday'
  if (days < 7) return `${days} days ago`
  if (days < 30) return `${Math.floor(days / 7)} weeks ago`
  return date.toLocaleDateString()
}

function getContentLink(item: WatchHistory): string {
  return `/${item.media_type}-${item.tmdb_id}`
}

function resumeWatching(item: WatchHistory) {
  const query: Record<string, string> = {
    tmdb: String(item.tmdb_id),
    type: item.media_type,
    title: item.title,
  }
  
  if (item.season_number && item.episode_number) {
    query.season = String(item.season_number)
    query.episode = String(item.episode_number)
  }
  
  router.push({
    path: '/watch',
    query,
  })
}

async function handleDelete(item: WatchHistory) {
  await deleteEntry(
    item.tmdb_id,
    item.season_number,
    item.episode_number
  )
  
  // Refresh data
  await fetchData()
}

onMounted(() => {
  fetchData()
})

// SEO
useHead({
  title: 'My List - Pichisfin',
  meta: [
    { name: 'description', content: 'View your watch history and continue watching' },
  ],
})
</script>

  @apply flex items-center gap-4 flex-1;
}

.history-poster {
  @apply w-16 h-24 flex-shrink-0;
}

.history-details {
  @apply flex-1;
}

.history-title {
  @apply text-white font-semibold mb-1;
}

.history-meta {
  @apply flex items-center gap-3 mb-2;
}

.history-episode,
.history-type,
.history-date {
  @apply text-netflix-lightGray text-sm;
}

.history-type {
  @apply uppercase;
}

.history-progress {
  @apply flex items-center gap-3;
}

.history-progress .progress-bar {
  @apply flex-1;
}

.history-delete {
  @apply text-netflix-gray hover:text-white transition-colors;
  @apply p-2;
}

.empty-state {
  @apply min-h-[50vh] flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center;
}

.empty-title {
  @apply text-white text-2xl font-bold mt-4;
}

.empty-text {
  @apply text-netflix-lightGray mt-2;
}
</style>

export default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {
  const config = useRuntimeConfig()
  const query = getQuery(event)
  
  const timeWindow = (query.time_window as 'day' | 'week') || 'day'
  const page = query.page || 1
  
  const url = new URL(`https://api.themoviedb.org/3/trending/all/${timeWindow}`)
  url.searchParams.set('api_key', config.public.tmdbApiKey)
  url.searchParams.set('language', config.public.tmdbLanguage || 'en')
  url.searchParams.set('page', String(page))
  
  try {
    const data = await $fetch(url.toString())
    return data
  } catch (error) {
    throw createError({
      statusCode: 500,
      statusMessage: 'Failed to fetch trending content',
    })
  }
})

```

  "content": "export default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {\n  const config = useRuntimeConfig()\n  const query = getQuery(event)\n  \n  const tmdb = query.tmdb as string\n  const type = (query.type as 'movie' | 'tv') || 'movie'\n  const season = query.season as string\n  const episode = query.episode as string\n  \n  if (!tmdb) {\n    throw createError({\n      statusCode: 400,\n      statusMessage: 'TMDB ID is required',\n    })\n  }\n  \n  let embedUrl: string\n  \n  if (type === 'movie') {\n    embedUrl = `${config.public.vidsrcBaseUrl}/embed/movie?tmdb=${tmdb}`\n  } else {\n    if (!season || !episode) {\n      throw createError({\n        statusCode: 400,\n        statusMessage: 'Season and episode are required for TV shows',\n      })\n    }\n    embedUrl = `${config.public.vidsrcBaseUrl}/embed/tv?tmdb=${tmdb}&season=${season}&episode=${episode}`\n  }\n  \n  return { embedUrl }\n})\n"

*Diff for `embed.get.ts`:*
```diff
export default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {
  const config = useRuntimeConfig()
  const query = getQuery(event)
  
  const tmdb = query.tmdb as string
  const type = (query.type as 'movie' | 'tv') || 'movie'
  const season = query.season as string
  const episode = query.episode as string
  
  if (!tmdb) {
    throw createError({
      statusCode: 400,
      statusMessage: 'TMDB ID is required',
    })
  }
  
  let embedUrl: string
  
  if (type === 'movie') {
    embedUrl = `${config.public.vidsrcBaseUrl}/embed/movie?tmdb=${tmdb}`
  } else {
    if (!season || !episode) {
      throw createError({
        statusCode: 400,
        statusMessage: 'Season and episode are required for TV shows',
      })
    }
    embedUrl = `${config.public.vidsrcBaseUrl}/embed/tv?tmdb=${tmdb}&season=${season}&episode=${episode}`
  }
  
  return { embedUrl }
})

```

````diff
# Pichisfin - Netflix Clone for Android TV

A Netflix-style streaming application optimized for Android TV, built with Nuxt 4, Supabase, and Vercel.

## Features

- 🎬 **Browse Movies & TV Shows** - Trending, popular, top rated content
- 🔍 **Search** - Powered by TMDB API
- ▶️ **Video Player** - iframe embed with vidsrc-embed.ru
- 📺 **Watch History** - Track progress and continue watching
- 📱 **Android TV Optimized** - D-pad navigation, focus management, 10-foot UX
- 🔐 **User Authentication** - Email/password + Google sign-in
- 🎨 **Netflix-style UI** - Dark theme, carousels, hero banners

## Tech Stack

- **Frontend**: Nuxt 4 (SPA mode)
- **Backend**: Supabase (PostgreSQL + Auth)
- **Video**: vidsrc-embed.ru (iframe embed)
- **Metadata**: TMDB API
- **Styling**: TailwindCSS
- **Deployment**: Vercel

## Prerequisites

- Node.js 18+
- npm or yarn
- Supabase account
- TMDB API key

## Setup

### 1. Clone & Install

```bash
npm install
```

### 2. Environment Variables

Copy `.env.example` to `.env` and fill in your credentials:

```bash
cp .env.example .env
```

Get your credentials:
- **Supabase**: Create project at https://supabase.com
- **TMDB API**: Get key at https://www.themoviedb.org/settings/api

### 3. Database Setup

Run the migration in your Supabase SQL editor:

```sql
-- Copy contents of supabase/migrations/001_initial_schema.sql
```

### 4. Development

```bash
npm run dev
```

### 5. Build for Production

```bash
npm run build
```

## Deployment

### Vercel

1. Push to GitHub
2. Import project on Vercel
3. Add environment variables
4. Deploy

## Project Structure

```
src/
├── assets/css/          # Styles
├── components/          # Vue components
│   ├── tv/             # TV-specific components
│   ├── common/         # Shared components
│   ├── auth/           # Authentication
│   ├── player/         # Video player
│   └── search/         # Search UI
├── composables/         # Logic hooks
├── layouts/             # Page layouts
├── middleware/          # Route middleware
├── pages/               # Route pages
├── server/api/          # API routes
└── types/               # TypeScript types
```

## Pages

- `/` - Home (trending, carousels)
- `/signin` - Sign in
- `/signup` - Sign up
- `/search` - Search content
- `/movie-[id]` - Movie details
- `/tv-[id]` - TV show details
- `/tv-[id]/season-[season]` - Season episodes
- `/watch` - Video player
- `/my-list` - Watch history

## Android TV Features

- D-pad navigation
- Focus management
- Large touch targets
- Optimized font sizes
- 10-foot UX design

## API Endpoints

### Server Routes

- `GET /api/tmdb/trending` - Trending content
- `GET /api/tmdb/search?q=query` - Search
- `GET /api/tmdb/movie/[id]` - Movie details
- `GET /api/tmdb/tv/[id]` - TV show details
- `GET /api/vidsrc/embed` - Generate embed URL

## License

MIT

````

*Diff for `TvCarousel.vue`:*
```diff
<template>
  <section v-if="items.length > 0" class="tv-carousel">
    <div class="carousel-header">
      <h2 class="carousel-title">{{ title }}</h2>
      <NuxtLink v-if="showViewAll" :to="viewAllLink" class="view-all-link">
        View All
      </NuxtLink>
    </div>
    
    <div class="carousel-content" ref="carouselRef">
      <button
        v-if="showLeftArrow"
        class="carousel-btn carousel-btn-left"
        @click="scrollLeft"
        @keydown.enter="scrollLeft"
        tabindex="0"
        aria-label="Scroll left"
      >
        <svg class="w-6 h-6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M15 19l-7-7 7-7" />
        </svg>
      </button>
      
      <div class="carousel-track" ref="trackRef">
        <slot :items="items"></slot>
      </div>
      
      <button
        v-if="showRightArrow"
        class="carousel-btn carousel-btn-right"
        @click="scrollRight"
        @keydown.enter="scrollRight"
        tabindex="0"
        aria-label="Scroll right"
      >
        <svg class="w-6 h-6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9 5l7 7-7 7" />
        </svg>
      </button>
    </div>
  </section>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
defineProps<{
  title: string
  showViewAll?: boolean
  viewAllLink?: string
}>()

const carouselRef = ref<HTMLElement | null>(null)
const trackRef = ref<HTMLElement | null>(null)

const showLeftArrow = ref(false)
const showRightArrow = ref(true)

const items = ref<any[]>([])

function updateArrowVisibility() {
  if (!trackRef.value) return
  
  const { scrollLeft, scrollWidth, clientWidth } = trackRef.value
  showLeftArrow.value = scrollLeft > 0
  showRightArrow.value = scrollLeft < scrollWidth - clientWidth - 10
}

function scrollLeft() {
  if (trackRef.value) {
    trackRef.value.scrollBy({ left: -400, behavior: 'smooth' })
  }
}

function scrollRight() {
  if (trackRef.value) {
    trackRef.value.scrollBy({ left: 400, behavior: 'smooth' })
  }
}

onMounted(() => {
  if (trackRef.value) {
    trackRef.value.addEventListener('scroll', updateArrowVisibility)
  }
})

onUnmounted(() => {
  if (trackRef.value) {
    trackRef.value.removeEventListener('scroll', updateArrowVisibility)
  }
})
</script>

<style scoped>
.tv-carousel {
  @apply py-6;
}

.carousel-header {
  @apply flex justify-between items-center px-4 md:px-8 mb-4;
}

.carousel-title {
  @apply text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold text-white;
}

.view-all-link {
  @apply text-netflix-lightGray text-sm hover:text-white transition-colors;
}

.carousel-content {
  @apply relative;
}

.carousel-track {
  @apply flex gap-3 overflow-x-auto;
  padding: 0 1rem;
  scroll-behavior: smooth;
  -webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch;
  scrollbar-width: none;
  -ms-overflow-style: none;
}

.carousel-track::-webkit-scrollbar {
  display: none;
}

.carousel-btn {
  @apply absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 z-10;
  @apply bg-netflix-dark bg-opacity-80 hover:bg-opacity-100;
  @apply text-white p-2 rounded-full transition-all duration-200;
  @apply focus:ring-4 focus:ring-white focus:ring-opacity-50;
}

.carousel-btn-left {
  @apply left-0 -translate-x-1/2;
}

.carousel-btn-right {
  @apply right-0 translate-x-1/2;
}

.carousel-btn:hover {
  transform: translateY(-50%) scale(1.1);
}
</style>

```

````diff
# Pichisfin - Netflix Clone for Android TV

A Netflix-style streaming application optimized for Android TV, built with Nuxt 4, Supabase, and Vercel.

## Features

- 🎬 **Browse Movies & TV Shows** - Trending, popular, top rated content
- 🔍 **Search** - Powered by TMDB API
- ▶️ **Video Player** - iframe embed with vidsrc-embed.ru
- 📺 **Watch History** - Track progress and continue watching
- 📱 **Android TV Optimized** - D-pad navigation, focus management, 10-foot UX
- 🔐 **User Authentication** - Email/password + Google sign-in
- 🎨 **Netflix-style UI** - Dark theme, carousels, hero banners

## Tech Stack

- **Frontend**: Nuxt 4 (SPA mode)
- **Backend**: Supabase (PostgreSQL + Auth)
- **Video**: vidsrc-embed.ru (iframe embed)
- **Metadata**: TMDB API
- **Styling**: TailwindCSS
- **Deployment**: Vercel

## Prerequisites

- Node.js 18+
- npm or yarn
- Supabase account
- TMDB API key

## Setup

### 1. Clone & Install

```bash
npm install
```

### 2. Environment Variables

Copy `.env.example` to `.env` and fill in your credentials:

```bash
cp .env.example .env
```

Get your credentials:
- **Supabase**: Create project at https://supabase.com
- **TMDB API**: Get key at https://www.themoviedb.org/settings/api

### 3. Database Setup

Run the migration in your Supabase SQL editor:

```sql
-- Copy contents of supabase/migrations/001_initial_schema.sql
```

### 4. Development

```bash
npm run dev
```

> **Note**: If you encounter dev server issues with Nuxt 4, use the production build instead:
> ```bash
> npm run build
> node .output/server/index.mjs
> ```

```bash

- **TMDB API**: Get key at https://www.themoviedb.org/settings/api

Run the migration in your Supabase SQL editor:

```sql
-- Copy contents of supabase/migrations/001_initial_schema.sql
```

### 4. Development

```bash
npm run dev
```

### 5. Build for Production

```bash
npm run build
```

## Deployment

### Vercel

1. Push to GitHub
2. Import project on Vercel
3. Add environment variables
4. Deploy

## Project Structure

```
src/
├── assets/css/          # Styles
├── components/          # Vue components
│   ├── tv/             # TV-specific components
│   ├── common/         # Shared components
│   ├── auth/           # Authentication
│   ├── player/         # Video player
│   └── search/         # Search UI
├── composables/         # Logic hooks
├── layouts/             # Page layouts
├── middleware/          # Route middleware
├── pages/               # Route pages
├── server/api/          # API routes
└── types/               # TypeScript types
```

## Pages

- `/` - Home (trending, carousels)
- `/signin` - Sign in
- `/signup` - Sign up
- `/search` - Search content
- `/movie-[id]` - Movie details
- `/tv-[id]` - TV show details
- `/tv-[id]/season-[season]` - Season episodes
- `/watch` - Video player
- `/my-list` - Watch history

## Android TV Features

- D-pad navigation
- Focus management
- Large touch targets
- Optimized font sizes
- 10-foot UX design

## API Endpoints

### Server Routes

- `GET /api/tmdb/trending` - Trending content
- `GET /api/tmdb/search?q=query` - Search
- `GET /api/tmdb/movie/[id]` - Movie details
- `GET /api/tmdb/tv/[id]` - TV show details
- `GET /api/vidsrc/embed` - Generate embed URL

   ```bash
   cp .env.example .env
   # Fill in your Supabase and TMDB credentials
   ```

2. **Set up Supabase database:**

   vercel login
   vercel deploy
   ```

To test on Android TV:
1. Use Android Studio's Android TV emulator
2. Or deploy to Vercel and access from your Android TV device
3. D-pad navigation is fully supported
